## 根因（一句话）

`data-theme="future"` 和 `future.css` 都是好的（截图里青色实心按钮就是 future.css 生效的证据），真正的问题是：**dev server 在 future 页面文件创建之前就启动了，Tailwind v4 的自动源文件扫描快照里根本没有 `src/pages/future/` 和 `src/layout/future/` 这两个新目录**，导致这些文件里所有 Tailwind 工具类（`bg-[#0a101c]`、`text-[#eef4fc]` 等）从未被生成，页面只剩下 body 的白色底 + future.css 里手写的那几个 `f-*` 类，于是深色主题整体失效、浅色文字落在白底上看不清。

诊断依据：curl 拉取 dev server 转换后的 `index.css`(70KB)，搜索 future 文件独有的颜色值 `0a101c / eef4fc / 22d3ee`,**命中 0 次**；而 POS 文件的同类工具类全部存在。重启 dev server 后重新拉取，`0a101c` 命中 2 次、`eef4fc` 4 次、`22d3ee` 22 次，工具类全部生成。

## 改了哪些文件

**没有改任何源码文件**——这是构建缓存问题，不是代码问题。唯一的动作：停掉旧 dev server 任务，以 strictPort 重启在 5174（后台任务，正在运行）。`index.css`、`future.css`、所有 future 页面组件经代码审查确认变量链完好（`[data-theme="future"]` 作用域覆盖 shadcn 变量，`@theme inline` 的运行时 `var()` 引用不受层叠顺序影响）。

## tsc 结果

`npx tsc -b`:**0 错误**。

## 修复后实际配色（截图自验，存在 /tmp/pwshot/)

- **`/f/login`**(f-login.png)：整页深海军蓝 `#0a101c` + 静态 aurora 渐变，居中玻璃登录卡（浅色描边+顶部高光）,caps 灰蓝标签、深色输入框、实心青荧光 `#22d3ee` 大按钮配深色文字，白色大标题。用脚本实测根容器 `backgroundColor = rgb(10, 16, 28)`，与设计令牌一致。
- **`/f/production`**(f-production.png)：深色侧栏（选中项青色描边+淡青底）、深色标签页（激活态青点+青描边）、单头玻璃卡内 mono 大号单号 `SC20260915003` 和数量 `1,020`、青色实心"打开"按钮+其余玻璃展示态按钮、青色步骤条节点、深色明细表配青色合计数字，所有文字均为 `#dbe4f0 / #eef4fc` 高对比浅色。
- **`/f/material-inventory`**(f-inventory.png)：三张 bento 玻璃卡（负库存卡检测出 4 条负库存，自动变红色调）、深色筛选栏、深色表格 mono 大数字，可读性良好。
- **POS 版零污染**(pos-login.png):`/login` 仍是浅色翡翠主题，左侧深色品牌区+右侧白色登录卡+翡翠按钮，与修复前完全一致。

## 用户操作与后续注意

让用户**硬刷新**(Cmd+Shift+R）即可看到效果。另外提醒：这是 Tailwind v4 的已知行为——**dev server 运行期间新建页面目录后，需要重启 dev server（或改动一下 index.css 触发重建）才会扫描到新文件的类**；以后给 web2 加新页面时注意这一点。截图用的 playwright-core 装在 /tmp/pwshot（隔离目录，未污染 web2 依赖），复用了 erp-tools/pw-browsers 里已有的 chromium-headless-shell，未下载新浏览器。

设计本身没问题，是构建缓存的锅——现在已修好，**硬刷新浏览器（Cmd+Shift+R）就能看到**。

原因：web2 的 dev server 是在未来版页面文件创建**之前**启动的，Tailwind v4 扫描快照里没有 `future/` 那两个新目录，深色背景、浅色文字这些样式类根本没生成，页面只剩白底+几个手写样式，所以白茫茫一片看不清。

处理：没有改任何代码（变量链审查完好），重启了 5174 的 dev server，并用无头浏览器截图自验过：

- `/f/login`：整页深海军蓝 `#0a101c` + 玻璃登录卡 + 青荧光实心按钮
- `/f/production`：深色侧栏/标签页、玻璃单头卡、mono 大字单号、深色明细表，全部高对比浅色文字
- `/f/material-inventory`：bento 玻璃卡正常，负库存卡自动变红色调
- POS 版（`/login` 等）确认零污染，仍是浅色翡翠风

一个后续注意点：Tailwind v4 在 dev server 运行期间新建页面目录后，需要重启 dev server 才会扫描到新文件的样式类——以后给 web2 加页面时我会留意。

## 改了哪些文件、哪些令牌

**问题 1：图标压住文字（根因：CSS 层叠优先级）**
`.f-input` 是无层叠（unlayered）普通 CSS，而 Tailwind v4 的工具类在 `@layer utilities` 里——无层叠样式优先级更高，所以 `pl-11` 被 `.f-input { padding: 0 14px }` 覆盖，左 padding 根本没生效。

- `src/future.css`：新增 `.f-input-icon { padding-left: 44px; }` 修饰类（与 `.f-input` 同优先级、写在其后，并注释说明为什么不能用 `pl-*` 工具类）
- `src/pages/future/FutureLoginPage.tsx`：两个输入框由 `f-input pl-11` 改为 `f-input f-input-icon`
- 排查了 future 版其他带图标输入框：顶栏搜索是 flex+gap 布局（非绝对定位图标，无此问题）、命令面板/"打开"对话框/库存关键字框均无内嵌图标，只有登录页受影响

**问题 2：系统性提亮文字令牌（6 个文件批量替换 + 变量升级）**

- `src/future.css`:`--muted-foreground: #7c8aa5 → #a8bad2`;`--secondary-foreground: #c6d2e4 → #d8e2f0`;`--f-text: #c6d2e4 → #d8e2f0`;`--f-text-dim: #7c8aa5 → #a8bad2`(`.f-label`、`.f-input` placeholder 等引用变量处自动生效）
- 批量替换硬编码值（FutureSidebar/FutureTopBar/FutureMainLayout/FutureLoginPage/FutureProductionPage/FutureInventoryPage):`#7c8aa5 → #a8bad2`（次要文字/图标，28 处）、`#c6d2e4 → #d8e2f0`（菜单项/正文次要文字，18 处）、`#46536b → #6f7f9d`（禁用/幽灵文字，10 处）

## 根因（那坨绿色是什么）

不是 aurora 漏进弹窗，也不是标题栏样式坏了，而是**弹窗整体尺寸失控把标题栏顶出了屏幕外**，两个叠加的样式冲突：

1. shadcn `DialogContent` 默认带 `sm:max-w-lg`(512px)，我传入的 `max-w-3xl` 与它在同一媒体查询下同优先级竞争输了，弹窗实际只有 **512px 宽** → 表头"客户款号"竖排、日期折行。
2. 弹窗没有 `max-h` 限制，10 行列表把内容撑到 **1002px 高**，居中定位后 top=-51px → **标题"打开生产通知单"和关闭 X 被顶到视口上方不可见，底部分页被裁到视口下方**。用户看到的"顶部绿色渐变块"就是标题栏消失后、从半透明遮罩透出来的页面 aurora 绿背景。

（用 playwright 实测 DOM 确认：title 和 close 按钮在文档里都存在，只是坐标在屏幕外；dialog rect 修复前 512×1002 top=-52，修复后 920×717 top=92。)

## 改了哪些文件

- `web2/src/pages/future/FutureProductionPage.tsx`(OpenDialog 重构）:`sm:max-w-[920px]`（同变体类名覆盖 shadcn 的 `sm:max-w-lg`，经 tailwind-merge 正确去重）、`flex flex-col max-h-[85vh] overflow-hidden p-0`；三段式结构——头部（标题+描述+搜索）固定、列表区 `flex-1 overflow-auto` 可滚、`thead sticky top-0` 白底固定、底部分页 `shrink-0 border-t` 固定可见；表格 `table-fixed` + colgroup 明确列宽（23/15/22/13/12/15%)，表头全部 `whitespace-nowrap`，日期/单号/数量单元格 nowrap，数量右对齐，客户名称 truncate+title 兜底。

## 根因

不是背景色没生效，而是 **sticky 加错了元素**：我把 `sticky top-0 z-10` 加在 `<thead>` 上，`bg-white` 加在 `<th>` 上。`position: sticky` 在 thead 上能定位（表头确实停住了），但 **table 盒模型内部元素（thead/tr）的 z-index 层叠在浏览器里不可靠**——滚动的 `<tbody>` 行内容绘制在 sticky thead 之上，而行与行之间、行单元格的透明区域让文字透出来，形成"表头镂空、第一行跑到表头上面"的叠影。headless Chromium 下也完整复现了（修复前截图 `sticky-repro.png` 可见 SC20260909002/09001 两行压在表头文字上），所以不是用户浏览器的问题。另外滚动容器有 12px 上 padding，行滚到表头上方的透明缝隙里也会露出一条。

## 改动文件

- `web2/src/pages/future/FutureProductionPage.tsx`（打开单据弹窗）:
  - sticky 从 `thead` 移到**每个 `th`** 上：`sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-black/8 bg-white`(th 是表格盒模型里 sticky 层叠最可靠的元素，且自带不透明背景，行内容无法穿透）
  - 滚动容器 `px-6 py-3` → `px-6 pb-3`（去掉顶部 12px 缝隙，th 贴住容器顶，行滚到顶即被不透明表头完全遮住）;tr 上的 border-b 移到 th 上（tr 边框不随 sticky 固定）
- `web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx`(POS 版同款弹窗）：同样两处修改，POS 配色未动。

**tsc**:`npx tsc -b` 0 错误。无 em/en dash、无 emoji。HMR 热载，硬刷新即可。

根因：固定表头的 `sticky` 加在了 `<thead>` 上，但表格内部元素的层叠顺序在浏览器里不可靠，滚动时数据行画到了表头上面，而行背景有透明区域，表头文字就透出来和行文字叠在一起——就是你看到的"镂空"。

修复：sticky 改加到每个表头单元格（`<th>`）上并带不透明白底+层级，同时去掉了滚动容器顶部的缝隙。我用小窗口强制列表滚动后截图验证过：滚走几行后表头白底完全不透、文字清晰压在行内容之上，底部"共 9 单，第 1/1 页"固定可见。POS 版同款弹窗有一样的问题，一并修了（配色没动）。

## 旧版功能点与 API 清单（来源：`web/src/pages/admin/AccountPage.tsx` + `web/src/api/admin.ts`)

功能点（6 项）:
1. 用户列表（用户/登录状态/上次登录/已锁定状态）+ 用户名关键字搜索
2. 注册账号（用户名 + 初始密码，需"保存"权限）
3. 重置密码（按用户，需"保存"权限）
4. 停用/启用账号（lock/unlock，需"功能"权限，Popconfirm 确认）
5. 删除账号（需"删除"权限，Popconfirm 确认）
6. 权限设置 Drawer：按"组"分组的菜单权限矩阵，9 个功能位（打开/保存/删除/打印/单价/金额/审核/反审核/功能）逐格勾选 + 全选行，保存生效

API（全部 curl 实测可用）:`GET /api/admin/accounts?keyword`、`POST /api/admin/accounts`（注册）、`POST /api/admin/accounts/{用户}/reset-password`、`POST .../lock`、`POST .../unlock`、`DELETE /api/admin/accounts/{用户}`、`GET /api/admin/accounts/{用户}/perms`（返回 146 个菜单 × 9 位）、`PUT .../perms`（保存）、`GET /api/admin/menus`（菜单目录）。旧版菜单位置：基础设置组 → 系统用户/用户权限（同页）。

## 新版页面结构与功能对齐

路由 `/f/accounts`（标签页"账号权限管理"),**6 项功能全部对齐，无丢失**:
- 用户表（白卡 + sticky th 模式：th 上 `sticky top-0 z-10` 不透明底、表头 nowrap)：头像圆（首字符）+ mono 用户名/登录状态/上次登录/状态徽章（正常绿、已锁定红）/操作列（权限、重置密码、停用|启用、删除红）
- 注册账号：居中弹窗（用户名+初始密码，提示"新账号默认无任何菜单权限")；重置密码：居中弹窗；停用/启用/删除：确认弹窗（删除/停用红色确认钮、启用绿色）；操作成功右下角轻提示 Toast
- 权限分配：右侧 800px 侧拉面板（遮罩点击/Esc 可关，不透明白底，头部"权限设置 + 账号 + 保存按钮"固定、矩阵区可滚、底部统计固定）,22 个权限组可折叠，组内表格 = 菜单 × 9 功能位绿色勾选框 + 全选列，保存调 PUT perms
- 菜单位置：与旧版一致放"基础设置"组——新增 `src/nav/futureMenu.ts`（在共享 MENU_TREE 上追加，共享文件不动，POS 侧栏零变化），宫格/部门侧栏/Ctrl+K 面板都切到 FUTURE_MENU_TREE
- 管理员可见控制：非 admin 登录时宫格中"系统用户"卡片置灰显示"仅管理员"不可点；直接访问 URL 时后端 403 → 页面显示"无权限访问"态

严格按用户三步（宫格点工程部 → 点卡片进 /f/production → 再点工程部）在干净浏览器会话中**没有复现白屏**：步骤 3 后 URL 正确回到 `/f`,main 区域正常渲染工程部卡片（DOM 实测 `children=1`，文本含"工程部/生产通知单/3 单待审核"),console 无任何报错。又追加了 4 个变体（窄轨模式、同部门连点两次、先点全部再点同部门、从账号页点部门）全部正常。

## 根因

用户侧是**长开浏览器标签页 + 我多轮热更新（HMR）造成的模块图不匹配**：用户测试期间我连续改了侧栏、首页、菜单数据，Vite HMR 局部热替换后，旧版首页组件持有的菜单数据（7 个组）和新版侧栏传来的部门 key(8 个组）可能对不上，首页按部门过滤后 `groups = []`，于是只渲染"工作台"标题、下面一大片全白——正是"主区域全白空白"且侧栏/顶栏/标签页都还在的形态。干净会话里模块图一致，所以复现不出来。硬刷新即可消除，但代码里这种"过滤为空就白"的分支不该存在。

# Writing Plans

## Overview

Write comprehensive implementation plans assuming the engineer has zero context for our codebase and questionable taste. Document everything they need to know: which files to touch for each task, code, testing, docs they might need to check, how to test it. Give them the whole plan as bite-sized tasks. DRY. YAGNI. TDD. Frequent commits.

Assume they are a skilled developer, but know almost nothing about our toolset or problem domain. Assume they don't know good test design very well.

**Announce at start:** "I'm using the writing-plans skill to create the implementation plan."

**Context:** If working in an isolated worktree, it should have been created via the `superpowers:using-git-worktrees` skill at execution time.

**Save plans to:** `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<feature-name>.md`
- (User preferences for plan location override this default)

## Scope Check

If the spec covers multiple independent subsystems, it should have been broken into sub-project specs during brainstorming. If it wasn't, suggest breaking this into separate plans — one per subsystem. Each plan should produce working, testable software on its own.

## File Structure

Before defining tasks, map out which files will be created or modified and what each one is responsible for. This is where decomposition decisions get locked in.

- Design units with clear boundaries and well-defined interfaces. Each file should have one clear responsibility.
- You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. Prefer smaller, focused files over large ones that do too much.
- Files that change together should live together. Split by responsibility, not by technical layer.
- In existing codebases, follow established patterns. If the codebase uses large files, don't unilaterally restructure - but if a file you're modifying has grown unwieldy, including a split in the plan is reasonable.

This structure informs the task decomposition. Each task should produce self-contained changes that make sense independently.

## Task Right-Sizing

A task is the smallest unit that carries its own test cycle and is worth a
fresh reviewer's gate. When drawing task boundaries: fold setup,
configuration, scaffolding, and documentation steps into the task whose
deliverable needs them; split only where a reviewer could meaningfully
reject one task while approving its neighbor. Each task ends with an
independently testable deliverable.

## Bite-Sized Task Granularity

**Each step is one action (2-5 minutes):**
- "Write the failing test" - step
- "Run it to make sure it fails" - step
- "Implement the minimal code to make the test pass" - step
- "Run the tests and make sure they pass" - step
- "Commit" - step

## Plan Document Header

**Every plan MUST start with this header:**

```markdown
# [Feature Name] Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** [One sentence describing what this builds]

**Architecture:** [2-3 sentences about approach]

**Tech Stack:** [Key technologies/libraries]

**Spec:** [path to the spec/design doc this plan implements — the plan
argues from the spec, so the spec travels with it; executors read both]

## Global Constraints

[The spec's project-wide requirements — version floors, dependency limits,
naming and copy rules, platform requirements — one line each, with exact
values copied verbatim from the spec. Every task's requirements implicitly
include this section.]

---
```

## Task Structure

````markdown
### Task N: [Component Name]

**Files:**
- Create: `exact/path/to/file.py`
- Modify: `exact/path/to/existing.py:123-145`
- Test: `tests/exact/path/to/test.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: [what this task uses from earlier tasks — exact signatures]
- Produces: [what later tasks rely on — exact function names, parameter
  and return types. A task's implementer sees only their own task; this
  block is how they learn the names and types neighboring tasks use.]

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```python
def test_specific_behavior():
    result = function(input)
    assert result == expected
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**

Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
Expected: FAIL with "function not defined"

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

```python
def function(input):
    return expected
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**

Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
Expected: PASS

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add tests/path/test.py src/path/file.py
git commit -m "feat: add specific feature"
```
````

## No Placeholders

Every step must contain the actual content an engineer needs. These are **plan failures** — never write them:
- "TBD", "TODO", "implement later", "fill in details"
- "Add appropriate error handling" / "add validation" / "handle edge cases"
- "Write tests for the above" (without actual test code)
- "Similar to Task N" (repeat the code — the engineer may be reading tasks out of order)
- Steps that describe what to do without showing how (code blocks required for code steps)
- References to types, functions, or methods not defined in any task

## Self-Review

After writing the complete plan, look at the spec with fresh eyes and check the plan against it. This is a checklist you run yourself — not a subagent dispatch.

**1. Spec coverage:** Skim each section/requirement in the spec. Can you point to a task that implements it? List any gaps.

**2. Placeholder scan:** Search your plan for red flags — any of the patterns from the "No Placeholders" section above. Fix them.

**3. Type consistency:** Do the types, method signatures, and property names you used in later tasks match what you defined in earlier tasks? A function called `clearLayers()` in Task 3 but `clearFullLayers()` in Task 7 is a bug.

If you find issues, fix them inline. No need to re-review — just fix and move on. If you find a spec requirement with no task, add the task.

## Execution Handoff

After saving the plan, offer execution choice:

**"Plan complete and saved to `docs/superpowers/plans/<filename>.md`. Two execution options:**

**1. Subagent-Driven (recommended)** - I dispatch a fresh subagent per task, review between tasks, fast iteration

**2. Inline Execution** - Execute tasks in this session using executing-plans, batch execution with checkpoints

**Which approach?"**

**If Subagent-Driven chosen:**
- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development
- Fresh subagent per task + two-stage review

**If Inline Execution chosen:**
- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:executing-plans
- Batch execution with checkpoints for review

# Subagent-Driven Development

Execute plan by dispatching a fresh implementer subagent per task, a task review (spec compliance + code quality) after each, and a broad whole-branch review at the end.

**Why subagents:** You delegate tasks to specialized agents with isolated context. By precisely crafting their instructions and context, you ensure they stay focused and succeed at their task. They should never inherit your session's context or history — you construct exactly what they need. This also preserves your own context for coordination work.

**Core principle:** Fresh subagent per task + task review (spec + quality) + broad final review = high quality, fast iteration

**Narration:** between tool calls, narrate at most one short line — the
ledger and the tool results carry the record.

**Continuous execution:** Do not pause to check in with your human partner between tasks. Execute all tasks from the plan without stopping. The only reasons to stop are the four named below, or all tasks complete. "Should I continue?" prompts and progress summaries waste their time — they asked you to execute the plan, so execute it.

**Rulings, not stalls.** A running plan does not wait on a human. Conflicts,
ambiguities, plan defects, a cap you would have asked to exceed — decide
them. The spec is the binding authority, the plan is its argument, and your
judgment settles what neither answers. Record every decision in the ledger as
`Ruling: <what you decided> — <why> — <what it costs if wrong>`, and keep
going. A wrong ruling costs rework your human partner can see and undo; a
session parked on a question costs their whole day and buys nothing.

Four things stop you, and only these: an irreversible or destructive
operation; a security-sensitive action; a side effect outside this worktree
that norms say you ask about first (a merge, a push to a shared branch, a
publish); and a plan so broken that every path forward is a guess. For those,
stop and ask.

## When to Use

```dot

    "Have implementation plan?" [shape=diamond];
    "Tasks mostly independent?" [shape=diamond];

    "Have implementation plan?" -> "Manual execution or brainstorm first" [label="no"];
    "Tasks mostly independent?" -> "Stay in this session?" [label="yes"];
    "Tasks mostly independent?" -> "Manual execution or brainstorm first" [label="no - tightly coupled"];
    "Stay in this session?" -> "subagent-driven-development" [label="yes"];
    "Stay in this session?" -> "executing-plans" [label="no - parallel session"];
}
```

**vs. Executing Plans (parallel session):**
- Same session (no context switch)
- Fresh subagent per task (no context pollution)
- Review after each task (spec compliance + code quality), broad review at the end
- Faster iteration (no human-in-loop between tasks)

## Setup

Ensure the work happens in an isolated workspace: use
superpowers:using-git-worktrees to create one or verify the existing one.
Never start implementation on a main/master branch without your human
partner's explicit consent.

Conversation memory does not survive compaction. In real sessions,
controllers that lost their place have re-dispatched entire completed task
sequences — the single most expensive failure observed. Track progress in
a ledger file, not only in todos.

- Each plan owns a workspace: at skill start, run this skill's
  `scripts/sdd-workspace PLAN_FILE` — it prints the plan's git-ignored
  directory (`<repo-root>/.superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>/`), home to
  every artifact for THIS plan: ledger, briefs, reports, review packages.
  Another plan's directory is never yours to read or write.
- Check for this plan's ledger at `<workspace>/progress.md`. If its first
  line names your plan file, tasks with a `Task <N>: complete` line are DONE
  — do not re-dispatch them; resume at the first task without one. A task
  whose last line is a fix round is mid-loop: resume the loop at the next
  round. A ledger whose first line names a different plan file — or a stray
  ledger at the old flat path `.superpowers/sdd/progress.md` — is another
  plan's progress: leave it in place and start your own, fresh.
- Create the ledger with its identity as the first line:
  `# SDD ledger — plan: <plan file path>`.
- The ledger is your recovery map: the commits it names exist in git even
  when your context no longer remembers creating them. After compaction,
  trust the ledger and `git log` over your own recollection.
- `git clean -fdx` will destroy the workspace (it's git-ignored scratch); if
  that happens, recover from `git log`.

Read the plan once, note its context and Global Constraints, and create a
todo per task. If the plan names a Spec, read that too: the spec is the
authority the plan argues from, and conflicts inside the plan resolve
against it. A plan with no reachable spec gets a ledger note saying so —
rulings made without one are provisional.

Before dispatching Task 1, scan the plan once for conflicts, writing down
what you checked as you check it:

- tasks that contradict each other or the plan's Global Constraints
- anything the plan explicitly mandates that the review rubric treats as a
  defect (a test that asserts nothing, verbatim duplication of a logic block)

The scan's output is a table, not a verdict. One row for every pair of tasks
that share a file or an interface: the two tasks, what one produces against
what the other consumes, and what you found. One row for every task: whether
its own text agrees with itself — the tests it specifies against the code it
specifies, the files it creates against the files it later touches. "The scan
is clean" without those rows is not a scan you ran.

Write the table to the ledger. Rule on everything you find before execution
begins — each finding against the plan text that mandates it — and record
each ruling in the ledger. If the scan is clean, proceed without comment.
Rule on each conflict it surfaces — the spec is the binding authority, the
plan is its argument — record the ruling beside its row, and dispatch
Task 1. The review loop remains the net for conflicts that only emerge from
implementation.

## Model Selection

Use the least powerful model that can handle each role to conserve cost and increase speed.

**Mechanical implementation tasks** (isolated functions, clear specs, 1-2 files): use a fast, cheap model. Most implementation tasks are mechanical when the plan is well-specified.

**Integration and judgment tasks** (multi-file coordination, pattern matching, debugging): use a standard model.

**Architecture and design tasks**: use the most capable available model.
The final whole-branch review is one of these — dispatch it on the most
capable available model, not the session default.

**Review tasks**: choose the model with the same judgment, scaled to the
diff's size, complexity, and risk. A small mechanical diff does not need the
most capable model; a subtle concurrency change does. Scoped re-reviews of
small fix diffs take a cheap-to-mid tier.

**Fix-loop escalation (rounds 4-5)**: use a model at least one tier above
the implementer that got stuck.

**Always specify the model explicitly when dispatching a subagent.** An
omitted model inherits your session's model — often the most capable and
most expensive — which silently defeats this section.

**Turn count beats token price.** Wall-clock and context cost scale with how
many turns a subagent takes, and the cheapest models routinely take 2-3× the
turns on multi-step work — costing more overall. Use a mid-tier model as the
floor for reviewers and for implementers working from prose descriptions.
When the task's plan text contains the complete code to write, the
implementation is transcription plus testing: use the cheapest tier for
that implementer. Single-file mechanical fixes also take the cheapest tier.

**Task complexity signals (implementation tasks):**
- Touches 1-2 files with a complete spec → cheap model
- Touches multiple files with integration concerns → standard model
- Requires design judgment or broad codebase understanding → most capable model

## The Task Loop

**Batch small same-shape work.** When the plan lists several tasks that are
each a small, independent edit of the same kind — the same one-line fix,
constant change, or field addition repeated across files — do not dispatch
one subagent per task. Compose ONE dispatch brief listing every file and
its change, send the whole batch to a single subagent, and review its diff
as one unit. Reserve one-dispatch-per-task for work that needs its own
judgment, its own tests, or its own review surface.

Everything you paste into a dispatch prompt — and everything a subagent
prints back — stays resident in your context for the rest of the session
and is re-read on every later turn. Hand artifacts over as files.

**Waiting on dispatched subagents:** never poll a wait interface with
short timeouts, and never sit in one silent, open-ended wait either.
While you have local work — ledger updates, packaging the next review,
reading reports — keep working; child results arrive on their own.
When you are genuinely idle, wait in bounded stretches (five to ten
minutes, where your platform allows), and between stretches post one
line of status and reconcile your live children: list them, and chase
any that finished without reporting. A bounded stretch keeps nearly
all of a long wait's efficiency while guaranteeing a stuck or lost
child is noticed within minutes, not at the end of the session.

### 1. Dispatch the implementer

Record BASE (`git rev-parse HEAD`) before dispatching — the review package
and fix-round diffs need it.

- **Task brief:** before dispatching an implementer, run this skill's
  `scripts/task-brief PLAN_FILE N` — it extracts the task's full text to a
  uniquely named file and prints the path. Compose the dispatch so the
  brief stays the single source of
  requirements. Your dispatch should contain: (1) one line on where this
  task fits in the project; (2) the brief path, introduced as "read this
  first — it is your requirements, with the exact values to use verbatim";
  (3) interfaces and decisions from earlier tasks that the brief cannot
  know; (4) your resolution of any ambiguity you noticed in the brief;
  (5) the report-file path and report contract. Exact values (numbers,
  magic strings, signatures, test cases) appear only in the brief. Never
  make a subagent read the whole plan file.
- **Report file:** name the implementer's report file after the brief
  (brief `…/task-N-brief.md` → report `…/task-N-report.md`) and put it in
  the dispatch prompt. The implementer writes the full report there and
  returns only status, commits, a one-line test summary, and concerns.
- A dispatch prompt describes one task, not the session's history. Do not
  paste accumulated prior-task summaries ("state after Tasks 1-3") into
  later dispatches — a real session's dispatch hit 42k chars of which 99%
  was pasted history. A fresh subagent needs its task, the interfaces it
  touches, and the global constraints. Nothing else.
- The dispatch carries the no-subagents contract (it is in the
  implementer template): the implementer never dispatches subagents —
  not helpers, and never a reviewer. Review arrives from you, after the
  report. In real sessions, every reviewer a worker spawned duplicated
  the task review the controller dispatched anyway — a full extra
  review seat per task.
- If an earlier task parked a finding in the area this task touches, carry
  a pointer to that ledger entry in the dispatch.
- Record the implementer's agent identity from the dispatch result —
  fix-loop rounds 1-3 resume this agent.
- Never dispatch multiple implementation subagents in parallel (conflicts).

Template: [implementer-prompt.md](implementer-prompt.md)

### 2. Handle the report

Implementer subagents report one of four statuses. Handle each appropriately:

**DONE:** Generate the review package (`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD`, from this skill's directory — it prints the unique file path it wrote; BASE is the commit you recorded before dispatching the implementer — never `HEAD~1`, which silently drops all but the last commit of a multi-commit task), then dispatch the task reviewer with the printed path.

**DONE_WITH_CONCERNS:** The implementer completed the work but flagged doubts. Read the concerns before proceeding. If the concerns are about correctness or scope, address them before review. If they're observations (e.g., "this file is getting large"), note them and proceed to review.

**NEEDS_CONTEXT:** The implementer needs information that wasn't provided. Provide the missing context and re-dispatch.

**BLOCKED:** The implementer cannot complete the task. Assess the blocker:
1. If it's a context problem, provide more context and re-dispatch with the same model
2. If the task requires more reasoning, re-dispatch with a more capable model
3. If the task is too large, break it into smaller pieces
4. If the plan itself is wrong, rule on the correction, ledger it, and re-dispatch with the ruling carried in the dispatch

**Never** ignore an escalation or force the same model to retry without changes. If the implementer said it's stuck, something needs to change.

If the implementer asks questions — before starting or mid-task — answer
clearly and completely, provide additional context if needed, and don't
rush it into implementation.

### 3. Review the task

Per-task reviews are task-scoped gates. The broad review happens once, at the
final whole-branch review. Never skip the task review, and never accept a
report missing either verdict — spec compliance AND task quality are both
required. Implementer self-review never replaces the task review; both are
needed.

- Hand the reviewer its diff as a file: run this skill's
  `scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD` and pass the reviewer the file path
  it prints (or, without bash: `git log --oneline`, `git diff --stat`,
  and `git diff -U10` for the range, redirected to one uniquely named
  file). The output never enters your own context, and the reviewer sees
  the commit list, stat summary, and full diff with context in one Read
  call. Use the BASE you recorded before dispatching the implementer —
  never `HEAD~1`, which silently truncates multi-commit tasks. Never
  dispatch a task reviewer without a diff file.
- **Reviewer inputs:** the task reviewer gets three paths — the same brief
  file, the report file, and the review package — plus the global
  constraints that bind the task.
- The global-constraints block you hand the reviewer is its attention
  lens. Copy the binding requirements verbatim from the plan's Global
  Constraints section or the spec: exact values, exact formats, and the
  stated relationships between components ("same layout as X", "matches
  Y"). The reviewer's template already carries the process rules (YAGNI,
  test hygiene, review method) — the constraints block is for what THIS
  project's spec demands.
- Do not add open-ended directives like "check all uses" or "run race tests
  if useful" without a concrete, task-specific reason
- Do not ask a reviewer to re-run tests the implementer already ran on the
  same code — the implementer's report carries the test evidence
- Do not pre-judge findings for the reviewer — never instruct a reviewer to
  ignore or not flag a specific issue. If you believe a finding would be a
  false positive, let the reviewer raise it and adjudicate it in the review
  loop. If the prompt you are writing contains "do not flag," "don't treat X
  as a defect," "at most Minor," or "the plan chose" — stop: you are
  pre-judging, usually to spare yourself a review loop.
The task reviewer may report "⚠️ Cannot verify from diff" items — requirements
that live in unchanged code or span tasks. These do not block the rest of the
review, but you must resolve each one yourself before marking the task
complete: you hold the plan and cross-task context the reviewer
lacks. If you confirm an item is a real gap, treat it as a failed spec
review — it enters the fix loop with the other findings.

Template: [task-reviewer-prompt.md](task-reviewer-prompt.md)

### 4. The fix loop

The loop triggers when the review reports spec ❌, any Critical or Important
finding, or a ⚠️ item you confirmed as a real gap.

Before the loop starts, two routes leave it immediately:

- Record Minor findings in the progress ledger as you go
  (`Task <N>: minor (deferred): <one-liner>`), and point the final
  whole-branch review at that list so it can triage which must be fixed
  before merge. A roll-up nobody reads is a silent discard. Minor findings
  never enter the loop.
- A finding labeled plan-mandated — or any finding that conflicts with
  what the plan's text requires — is yours to rule on: weigh the finding
  against the plan text, decide with the spec as the binding authority, and
  ledger the ruling before you act on it. Do not dismiss the finding because
  the plan mandates it, and do not dispatch a fix that contradicts the plan
  without a recorded ruling.
Everything else enters the loop. A fix round is one fix dispatch plus one
scoped re-review. Five rounds maximum per task:

**Rounds 1-3 — resume the original implementer.** Send it the open findings
verbatim. Its context is intact: it knows the task, the code, and its own
choices. If your harness cannot send another message to a live subagent,
dispatch a fresh implementer carrying the brief path, the report-file path,
and the findings — the report file is the persistent memory either way.

**Rounds 4-5 — dispatch a fresh implementer on a more capable model** (per
Model Selection), with the brief path, the report-file path, the open
findings, and this framing: "A prior implementer attempted this task
[N] times; you own it now. Read the report file for what was tried." A loop
that survives three resumes usually means the implementer cannot see its
own problem — fresh eyes and a capability bump in one move.

**Every round, either way:** the implementer fixes, re-runs the tests
covering the amended code, appends its fix report to the same report file,
and returns the short contract. Before re-dispatching the reviewer, confirm
the fix report contains the covering tests, the command run, and the
output; dispatch the re-review once all three are present. Name the
covering test files in the fix message — a one-line fix does not need the
whole suite.

**The re-review is scoped.** Run `scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE FIX_BASE HEAD`
where FIX_BASE is the head the previous review saw, and dispatch
[re-review-prompt.md](re-review-prompt.md) with the findings list, the
brief, the report file, and the printed diff path. The re-reviewer verdicts
each finding ADDRESSED or NOT ADDRESSED and flags new breakage in the fix
diff only. New Critical/Important breakage in the fix diff joins the open
findings list. Out-of-scope observations go to the ledger as deferred
minors — they never extend the loop.

**After each round,** append to the ledger:
`Task <N>: fix round <R>/5 (<X> addressed, <Y> open — <finding one-liners>; commits <a7>..<b7>)`

Never fix findings yourself in the controller session — your context stays
clean for coordination, and controller fixes skip review.

**The breaker.** When round 5's re-review still leaves findings open, stop
dispatching. Adjudicate each open finding yourself — you hold the plan and
the cross-task context the reviewer lacks:

- **The reviewer is wrong, or the point is contestable:** park it —
  `Task <N>: parked — <finding> — Ruling: <why the code stands>`. The final
  review sees both sides.
- **Real, but nothing downstream builds on it:** park it the same way, with
  a ruling that says it's real and deferred.
- **Real and load-bearing** — a later task builds on it, or it reveals a
  plan defect: rule on the smallest change that unblocks the dependent work,
  ledger it as `Task <N>: Ruling: <finding> — <what you decided and why>`,
  and carry it into the next task's dispatch. Parking a structural failure
  silently lets every dependent task build on it. Stop only when the defect
  leaves every path forward a guess.

Adjudicate only at the cap. Adjudicating earlier to end a loop is
pre-judging with a different name. Every adjudication is a ledger entry —
a silent discard is forbidden.

### 5. Complete the task

When the review comes back clean — or every open finding is parked with a
ruling at the cap — append the completion line to the ledger in the same
message as your other bookkeeping:

- `Task <N>: complete (commits <base7>..<head7>, review clean)`
- `Task <N>: complete (commits <base7>..<head7>, <K> parked)` after a
  tripped breaker

Then mark the todo complete and move on. Never move to the next task while
the review has open Critical/Important issues that are neither fixed nor
parked-with-ruling at the cap.

## Final Review

The final whole-branch review gets a package too: run
`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE MERGE_BASE HEAD` (MERGE_BASE = the commit the
branch started from, e.g. `git merge-base main HEAD`) and include the
printed path in the final review dispatch, so the final reviewer reads
one file instead of re-deriving the branch diff with git commands. Dispatch
on the most capable available model (see Model Selection), using
superpowers:requesting-code-review's
[code-reviewer.md](../requesting-code-review/code-reviewer.md). Point it at
the ledger's deferred-minor and parked lines so it can triage which must be
fixed before merge.

If the final whole-branch review returns findings, dispatch ONE fix subagent
with the complete findings list — not one fixer per finding.
Per-finding fixers each rebuild context and re-run suites; a real
session's final-review fix wave cost more than all its tasks combined.
Then run exactly one scoped re-review of the fix wave
(`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE FIX_BASE HEAD` over the fix range,
[re-review-prompt.md](re-review-prompt.md)).
Adjudicate any residual findings as in the task loop's breaker: park with
rulings, or rule on the load-bearing ones and ledger what you decided. Only
the four classes above stop you here. There is no second fix wave —
residual load-bearing findings surface to your human partner when
finishing-a-development-branch presents the options.

## Finish

Before you delete anything, collect every ledger line containing `Ruling:` —
preflight rulings, parked findings, breaker adjudications, all of them — into
your final message under "Rulings I made", in the order you made them, each
with what it costs if wrong. The list is exhaustive: if the ledger holds a
ruling, the list holds it. That list is the only place the decisions you
took on your human partner's behalf reach them — they read it and rework
whatever you got wrong. A ruling that dies with the workspace was a decision
made in secret.

When the final whole-branch review is clean and its fixes are merged,
delete this plan's workspace (`rm -rf <workspace>`) — the git history is
the record now. Sibling directories belong to other plans; leave them
alone.

Use superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch.

## Common Rationalizations

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Close enough on spec compliance" | Reviewer found spec gaps = not done. Fix or hit the cap and adjudicate — those are the only exits. |
| "I'll fix it myself, dispatching is overhead" | Controller fixes pollute your context and skip review. Resume the implementer. |
| "One more round will converge" | Past the cap, rounds don't converge — the failure is structural. Adjudicate and route. |
| "The reviewer will just find something new anyway" | Scoped re-reviews verify fixes; they cannot wander. New findings on untouched code go to the ledger, not the loop. |
| "This finding is obviously wrong, I'll drop it" | You adjudicate only at the cap, and every ruling is a ledger entry. Silent discards are forbidden. |
| "The fix was small, skip the re-review" | Unreviewed fixes are how regressions land. Every round ends with a scoped re-review. |
| "Reviews slow the loop down" | The loop without reviews is just unverified churn. Reviews are the loop's brakes and steering. |
| "Ledger bookkeeping is overhead" | The ledger is what survives compaction. Controllers without one have re-dispatched entire completed task sequences. |
| "The implementer spawned its own reviewer — free extra assurance" | It's a duplicate seat reviewing the same diff; the task review is the gate. A worker-spawned reviewer is a defect to flag, not rigor. |

## Example Workflow

```
You: I'm using Subagent-Driven Development to execute this plan.

[Setup: worktree verified]
[Read plan file once: docs/superpowers/plans/feature-plan.md]
[Resolve workspace: scripts/sdd-workspace docs/superpowers/plans/feature-plan.md — no ledger inside, fresh start]
[Create todos for all tasks]

Task 1: Hook installation script

[Run task-brief for Task 1; dispatch implementer with brief + report paths + context]

Implementer: "Before I begin - should the hook be installed at user or system level?"

You: "User level (~/.config/superpowers/hooks/)"

Implementer: [Later]
  - Implemented install-hook command
  - Added tests, 5/5 passing
  - Self-review: Found I missed --force flag, added it
  - Committed

[Run review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD; dispatch task reviewer with the printed path]
Task reviewer: Spec ✅ - all requirements met, nothing extra.
  Strengths: Good test coverage, clean. Issues: None. Task quality: Approved.

[Ledger: Task 1: complete (commits a1b2c3d..d4e5f6a, review clean)]

Task 2: Recovery modes

[Run task-brief for Task 2; dispatch implementer with brief + report paths + context]

Implementer: [No questions]
  - Added verify/repair modes
  - 8/8 tests passing
  - Committed

[Run review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD; dispatch task reviewer with the printed path]
Task reviewer: Spec ❌:
  - Missing: Progress reporting (spec says "report every 100 items")
  Issues (Important): Magic number (100)

[Fix round 1: resume the implementer with both findings]
Implementer: Added progress reporting, extracted PROGRESS_INTERVAL constant.
  Re-ran test/recovery.test.js — 10/10 passing. Fix report appended.

[Run review-package PLAN_FILE FIX_BASE HEAD; dispatch scoped re-review]
Re-reviewer: Missing progress reporting — ADDRESSED (src/recovery.js:41).
  Magic number — ADDRESSED (src/recovery.js:7). New breakage: none.
  Verdict: all findings addressed.

[Ledger: Task 2: fix round 1/5 (2 addressed, 0 open; commits d4e5f6a..b7c8d9e)]
[Ledger: Task 2: complete (commits d4e5f6a..b7c8d9e, review clean)]

...

[After all tasks]
[Run review-package PLAN_FILE MERGE_BASE HEAD; dispatch final code-reviewer, most capable model]
Final reviewer: All requirements met. Deferred minors triaged: none block merge.

[Delete this plan's workspace — the record now lives in git]

Done! Using superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch.
```

## 审查结论：Task 1（定版基线 + vitest 测试基座，d3dea7b）

### Spec 合规：✅（一个流程性遗留，见 Important-1）

逐条核对 brief：

- Step 1 测试依赖：✅ `package.json` 加了 vitest/jsdom/testing-library 三件套/user-event，`test`/`test:watch` scripts 就位；node_modules 实测已装。
- Step 2 vitest 基座：✅ `vitest.config.ts` 与 brief 一致（多补了 `@` alias，与 vite.config.ts 对齐，合理）；`renderWithProviders` 签名与 brief 完全一致。文件名 `setup.ts`→`setup.tsx` 偏离 brief，但 brief 模板本身在 `.ts` 里写 JSX 会被 oxc 拒绝，实施者的偏离有正当理由且已在报告说明。
- Step 3 冒烟测试：✅ `shell.test.tsx` 是真实断言（`findByText("全部")` + `getByText("工程部")`，非空断言），token key `web2.token` 与 src/lib/auth.ts 一致。
- Step 4 future 转正 + POS 退役：✅ 路由表 `/login` + `/*`（`/production`、`/material-inventory`、`/accounts` 在 MainLayout PAGES 表登记）；POS 三页面与 POS 布局五件套、仅 POS 引用的 6 个组件全部删除；`git show` 逐字比对确认 ProductionPage/LoginPage/MainLayout/TopBar 是 Future 版的忠实移植（差异仅组件名、`/f` 前缀、注释）；登录成功 `navigate("/")`；「回旧版」指向 `http://localhost:5173`。
- Step 5 契约 smoke：✅ `contract.test.ts` 有真实断言（`res.ok` + `body.令牌`），后端不在时 skip。
- Step 6 截图自验：❌ 未做（见 Important-1）。

无多做（YAGNI 清白）：lucide→phosphor 替换和 lucide-react 卸载是全局约束要求的，不算多做；index.css 令牌删除是 brief 明文要求；MainErrorBoundary 等看似"新增"的代码经 `git show` 比对确认全部来自被删的 FutureMainLayout，非新写。

### 独立验证结果（Bash/Grep 实测，非采信报告）

- `grep -rn "lucide" src/` → 0 命中；package.json、package-lock.json 均无 lucide；`node_modules/lucide-react` 已不存在。真卸载 ✅
- `grep -rn "Future" src/` → 0 命中；`"/f`、`'/f` 路由残留 0；`src/pages/future`、`src/layout/future` 目录已不存在 ✅
- 无 em/en dash、无 emoji（ripgrep 全扫 src/）✅
- `var(--f-*)` 无兜底写法 0 命中 ✅
- 被删文件（MaterialInventoryPage/SidebarNav/CommandPalette/PAGE_TITLES/futureMenu/ui-card 等）在 src/ 无任何残留引用 ✅
- 被删令牌（bg-card/text-success/chart-*/sidebar-* 等）在 src/ 无任何残留 class 使用 ✅
- `git diff --name-only 0fb4fd3..d3dea7b` 中非 `web2/` 文件 = 0，web/ 与后端零改动 ✅

### 发现

**Critical：无**

**Important**

1. **Step 6 未执行即提交检查点** — brief 明确要求「playwright 截 `/login`、`/`、`/production` 三张确认白绿主题无损；报告用户，**经确认后**提交检查点」。实施者跳过截图和用户确认直接 commit（报告遗留问题 1 已自报）。代码层面移植忠实度高、风险低，但「白绿主题无损」这一验收点目前无任何证据。建议补一次截图自验后再放行下一任务。

**Minor**

1. `web2/src/test/contract.test.ts:16` — 测试名「登录拿 token 并拉到未读数」但实际只断言登录响应，从未拉未读数（reachable() 里的 unread-count 只是探活）。偏差源自 brief 模板本身，实施者照抄，建议后续改名或补断言。
2. `web2/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx`（登录按钮）— 绿色主按钮仍用 `f-btn-cyan` 类名，`future.css:137` 注释承认「类名沿用，仅换配色」。命名遗留，无害。
3. `web2/src/layout/DeptSidebar.tsx:8` — localStorage key `web2.f.deptCollapsed`→`web2.deptCollapsed`，用户折叠偏好重置一次。一次性影响，报告已说明，可接受。
4. lucide→phosphor 等价性核查通过：Check/Search/X/ChevronRight/Circle/ChevronDown/ChevronUp → Check/MagnifyingGlass/X/CaretRight/Circle/CaretDown/CaretUp，别名保留原变量名、JSX 零改动，phosphor 图标接受相同 className props。替换等价 ✅（此条为对实施者 concern 的回应，非缺陷）。
5. portal 弹层吃 `:root` 翡翠绿（oklch(0.475 0.093 168.5) ≈ #0d7a5f）与 future 主色 #16a34a 的色差：本任务不修是**合理判断** —— brief 只要求「删仅 POS 使用的令牌、保留 future.css 引用」，未要求统一 portal 主色；且 index.css 注释已把 `:root` 重新定位为「portal 弹层兜底」，语义自洽。建议列为后续任务：把 `:root --primary/--accent/--ring` 对齐 future 绿。

### 无法从 diff 验证的项 ⚠️

- `npx vitest run` 2 文件全过、`tsc -b` 0 错、`oxlint` 0 err/6 warning —— 按指示未重跑。间接佐证：无任何悬空 import/引用（tsc 若不过必然有残留引用报错），oxlint 报告所列 6 个 warning 均在移动前既存代码的未改动行上，「既有」说法可信。
- 视觉回归（白绿主题无损）—— 无截图证据，见 Important-1。
- 契约测试真实命中后端（报告说 localhost:5000 在线跑通）—— 未复核后端当时状态。

### 代码质量：Approved

移植忠实（`git show` 逐字比对 4 个改写文件，差异仅改名/路由/注释，无夹带行为变更）；删除彻底且经全仓 grep 验证无残留；测试有真实断言；全局约束（无 dash/emoji、phosphor 唯一图标源、web/ 与后端零改动、f-* 兜底）逐条实测通过。唯一扣分项是 Step 6 流程未走完，属流程问题而非代码质量问题，且实施者已诚实自报。建议：补截图自验，并把 portal 主色对齐、contract 测试名实不符两项记入后续任务清单。

</details>

# Task 2 审查结论

## Spec 合规（对照 brief 逐条）

- ✅ **Files**:7 组件（`components/doc/`)+ `hooks/usePerms.ts` + `hooks/useDocTabs.ts` + `__tests__/docSkeleton.test.tsx` 全部创建；`ProductionPage.tsx` 修改；commit stat 确认只动 web2，老工程 web/ 与后端零改动
- ✅ **DocAction/DocToolbar 签名**：与 brief 逐字一致，扩展位 `success`、`menuKey` 均为可选；不传时 `toneClass` 落到 primary/danger/默认分支，行为与 brief 一致，向后兼容成立
- ✅ **FlowSteps**:`{ steps: string[]; current: number }` 逐字一致
- ✅ **HeaderField/DocHeaderCard**：逐字一致
- ✅ **OpenDocDialog<T>**：前 8 个 prop 与 brief 逐字一致；`description/loading/error/onRetry/onSearch/emptyHint` 全可选，缺省行为（前端过滤、无说明行、直接渲染表格）与 brief 语义一致，向后兼容成立
- ✅ **usePerms/useDocTabs**：签名逐字一致；usePerms 确为 TanStack Query + staleTime 5min(`usePerms.ts:14`)
- ✅ **Step 1 测试**：三断言齐备且为真实断言（权限过滤用 stub fetch + `waitFor` 否定断言；FlowSteps 断类名与 svg 有无；th 断 `sticky top-0 z-10` + `bg-white`)，用了 `renderWithProviders` + `vi.stubGlobal("fetch")`，与 brief 指定手法一致
- ✅ **全局约束**:grep 实证 web2/src 无 em/en dash、无 emoji、无 lucide 残留；图标全 @phosphor；新组件未用 `var(--f-*)`(future.css 内既有引用均带兜底）；硬编码 hex 全部能在 future.css 令牌表中找到对应值；弹窗四律满足（`bg-[#ffffff]`、`max-h-[85vh]`、th 上 `sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white`、`whitespace-nowrap`)
- ⚠️ **重构纪律**：逐函数比对 FlowBar/Field/ActionButton/FEmpty/FError/OpenDialog 的删除侧与骨架组件的新增侧，类名、文案、tone 映射、查询 key、分页逻辑、空值守卫均一一对应——**除一处边界行为差异**（见 Minor 2)

## 发现

**Important**

1. `web2/src/hooks/useDocTabs.ts:25-26` — `closeTab` 逻辑错误：`TabsContext.close()` 仅在关闭的是**当前激活**标签时返回相邻 key，关闭非激活标签也返回 `null`(`TabsContext.tsx:41-44`);`navigate(next ?? "/")` 把两种情况混为一谈，关闭一个后台标签会把用户错误地拽回首页。该 hook 是后续 9 个任务的共享地基，目前虽无人调用（死代码）无运行时影响，但以错误实现入库会把 bug 传播给后续任务。修复方式：close 前先记录 `active === path`，仅当关闭的是激活标签时才 navigate。

**Minor**

2. `web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx:409-412` — 边界行为变更：旧代码 `onClick={() => h.生产单号 && onPick(h.生产单号)}`，点空 `生产单号` 的行时弹窗保持打开；新代码 `if (h.生产单号) set单号(...); setDialogOpen(false)` 无条件关弹窗。`生产单号` 是行主键实际不会为空，截图对比也抓不到，但严格说违反了"行为不许变"。报告里"空值守卫保留"的说法只对了一半。
3. `web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx:177` — `useMemo<ColumnDef<ProductionHeader, any>[]>` 显式 `any`(accessor 列值类型异构的常用逃避，可用 `ColumnDef<ProductionHeader, unknown>` 或省略泛型让 helper 推导）。
4. 报告表述失实：报告称 usePerms"菜单行缺失时严格拒绝（测试覆盖）"，但 `docSkeleton.test.tsx` 只有 3 个测试，覆盖的是"行存在但位为 false"，"菜单行缺失"分支无测试。
5. 信息项：`DocToast`、`useDocTabs` 当前无人使用（grep 仅自身文件命中），属 brief 范围内的先行交付，非缺陷；`MENU_TREE` 中"生产通知单"在工程部/装配部重复但 label 相同，`menuKeyForPath` 取首个无影响。

## ⚠️ 无法从 diff 验证项

- vitest 5 过 / tsc 0 错 / oxlint 6 warning 持平（实施者自报，按指示未重跑）
- playwright 四场景 0 像素差异（截图与脚本在 sdd/shots/，未进 git)
- 弹窗在真实浏览器中 Radix portal 渲染、sticky 表头滚动行为

## 结论

- **Spec 合规：✅**（接口签名逐字一致、扩展位全部向后兼容、约束扫描干净；Minor 2 的边界偏差不构成 spec 失败）
- **代码质量：不 Approved** — 仅需修 Important 1(`useDocTabs.closeTab` 的误导航）即可转 Approved;ProductionPage 重构本身质量高：组件单一职责、提取忠实原样、注释解释了 minSize=0 与 sticky-on-th 两个非显然决策，测试断言真实。

复核完成。以下是逐项裁决。

## 发现 1(Important,useDocTabs.closeTab 误导航）—— NOT ADDRESSED

**diff 内的修复本身是正确的**（针对被引用行）:

- `web2/src/hooks/useDocTabs.ts:27-29` — `close(path)` 前先记录 `wasActive = active === path`，仅 `wasActive` 时才 `navigate(next ?? "/")`，逻辑与要求一致；`TabsContext.close` 确实只对关闭激活标签返回相邻 key、后台标签返回 `null`(`web2/src/layout/TabsContext.tsx:41-44`)，与注释吻合。
- 两个新测试（`web2/src/__tests__/docTabs.test.tsx:36-60`）是真实断言且覆盖正反两路：关激活 `/b` → 断言 `loc` 变为 `/a`；关后台 `/a` → 断言 `loc` 停留 `/b`。用 userEvent 实际点击、断言路由与标签列表，非空断言。

**但该发现的用户可见缺陷并未消除**：`useDocTabs` 在生产代码中**没有任何调用方**（全仓 grep 仅命中 hook 自身和新测试）。真实 UI 的关闭按钮走的是 `MainLayout` 里 `TabBar` 自己的路径：

- `web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:93` — 关闭按钮 `onClosed(close(t.key))`
- `web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:149` — `onClosed={(next) => navigate(next ?? "/")}`

关闭后台标签时 `close()` 返回 `null`，于是 `navigate(null ?? "/")` → 照样把用户拽回首页（`showHome` 判定后渲染宫格首页，MainLayout.tsx:130,152）。即发现描述的"关非激活标签被拽回首页"在真实操作路径上原样复现。修复加在了一个死代码 hook 上，验收要求"仅关闭激活标签时才导航"作为应用行为未达成。修复方向应该是：让 `TabBar` 的 `onClosed` 只在关闭激活标签时导航（或在 `close` 返回处区分），或直接复用修好后的 `closeTab`。

## 发现 2(Minor 4,usePerms 缺失行测试）—— ADDRESSED

- `web2/src/__tests__/docSkeleton.test.tsx` 新增用例（diff 行 64-82，落盘文件同位置）:stub 权限接口只返回「系统用户」行，在 `/production` 渲染 `DocToolbar`(`menuKeyForPath("/production")` → "生产通知单",`web2/src/nav/menu.ts:39`),`waitFor` 断言 `queryByText("打开")` 不在文档中。真实走通 `usePerms.ts:25` 的"菜单行缺失 → false"分支和 `DocToolbar.tsx:75` 的过滤，加载完成后按钮从"乐观放行"消失也有 `waitFor` 兜底，断言真实。

## 检查点补充

- 新增 `afterEach(cleanup)`(docSkeleton)：每个用例独立 `renderWithProviders`，不依赖跨用例 DOM；显式 cleanup 不改变既有测试语义，与 docTabs.test.tsx 的 `afterEach(cleanup)` 一致。无影响。
- diff 内新破坏：无 Critical/Important。仅一条 Minor（低于报告门槛，仅备注）:`useDocTabs.ts:6` 头注释仍写"closeTab 关闭后跳到相邻标签，无标签则回首页"，与"仅激活时导航"的新行为不符。

## 总结论

**仍有 open**。发现 2 已解决；发现 1 的 diff 修改与测试在被引用的 hook 上是正确的，但真实关闭路径（`MainLayout.tsx:93,149`）绕过了该 hook，原缺陷仍可复现，裁决 NOT ADDRESSED，需要针对 `TabBar` 的 `onClosed` 再修一轮（建议顺手让 `TabBar` 复用 `useDocTabs.closeTab`，消除两份关闭逻辑）。

</details>

## 1. (Important) MainLayout 真实关闭路径误导航 —— ADDRESSED

- **TabBar 关闭按钮走 useDocTabs.closeTab**：关闭按钮 onClick 为 `closeTab(t.key)`(`MainLayout.tsx:85-88`)，激活点击走 `openTab(t.key, t.title)`(`MainLayout.tsx:77`);`TabBar` 无 props，从 `useDocTabs()` 取 `tabs/openTab/closeTab`(`MainLayout.tsx:56-58`)。
- **新测试真实触达 TabBar 关闭按钮，非只测 hook**：测试从 `@/layout/MainLayout` 导入真实 `TabBar` 组件渲染（`web2/src/__tests__/docTabs.test.tsx:24,73`)，用 `screen.getByLabelText("关闭 生产通知单")` 点击——该 aria-label 正是真实关闭按钮的 `aria-label={关闭 ${t.title}}`(`MainLayout.tsx:83`)。两断言符合要求：
  - 关后台标签→标签移除且路由停留 `/inventory` 不跳首页（`docTabs.test.tsx:88-99`);
  - 关激活标签→跳到相邻 `/production`(`docTabs.test.tsx:101-109`)。
- 报告声称 `npx vitest run` 10 测试全过 + playwright 真实栈冒烟 PASS；按约定未重跑测试，但测试代码本身经逐行核对，逻辑与真实 UI 路径一致，且 hook 的 `wasActive` 守卫与 `TabsContext.close()` 仅激活时返回 next 的契约（`TabsContext.tsx:41-44`）自洽。

## 2. (Minor) useDocTabs.ts:6 头注释 —— ADDRESSED

`web2/src/hooks/useDocTabs.ts:6-7` 现为「closeTab 仅当关闭的是激活标签时才导航（跳到相邻标签，无标签则回首页），关闭后台标签只移除、停留在当前路由」，与 `closeTab` 实现（`useDocTabs.ts:26-33`）逐字对应。

# Task 3 审查结论：生产通知单全量对齐

## Spec 合规（逐条，对照需求书 Step 1)

| 场景 | 判定 | 依据 |
|---|---|---|
| 新建（清空/正单/一行空货号） | ✅ | production.test.tsx「点『新建』进入新建态」真实断言表单值 |
| 保存创建（回落逻辑/空行过滤） | ✅ | POST 载荷逐字段断言：货号回落 BOM款号、接单数量回落合计、数量明细过滤 |
| 保存修改（PUT，货号明细传 []) | ✅ | 断言 `货号明细` 为 `[]`、跟单员新值 |
| 删除（未审核确认删/已审核禁删） | ✅ | 两条 it，确认弹窗文案 + DELETE URL + 已审核无按钮均真实断言 |
| 三级审核流转 | ✅（带 ⚠️) | 后端 ProductionController 仅单级 `/approve`，与老页面一致；报告已如实披露，FlowSteps 做展示映射。不算 spec 缺失 |
| 申请反审核（必填原因/整步含BOM/单个） | ✅ | 3 条 it,payload 断言 `{原因, 含BOM: true/false}`，与后端 `ProductionDtos.cs:111`、老系统 `web/src/api/production.ts:90` 字段一致 |
| 打印字段契约 | ✅ | 已核实 `web/src/__tests__/printContracts.test.ts` 三模板确属采购/装配/塑胶单据，不含生产通知单；老页面 `ProductionNoticePage.tsx:415` 也是 `window.print()`，报告说明属实 |
| 明细行编辑/比例/分析 | ✅ | 回填、色码行增删改、数量列回合计、比例可改均断言 |
| 展开更多字段 | ✅ | 已审核只读卡内断言（审核人=经理） |
| BOM-PO 绑定确认弹窗 | ✅（1 个 Important 缺口） | 见下 |
| 权限脱敏 | ✅ | 3 条 it：无单价/金额位时 `***` >= 4 处且无 "HK 12.5"/"500" 泄漏；有位时正常显示；无删除位不渲染按钮。真实断言非快照 |
| 范围外未做项如实列出 | ✅ | 报告「遗留」1/2 列明一键启动/下推领料/MO单/图片备注/工序物料页签/BOM 实时预览，另如实披露 datalist 替代 AutoComplete、DocToolbar tooltip、usePerms admin 端点 |

## 重点核查结果

- **BOM-PO 绑定**：测试真实断言文案全串 `已绑定 PO:PO-OTHER、PO-OTHER2,是否继续使用?`、两个 PO 号列出、**未确认前 BOM款号 为空**、点「继续使用」后采用（双绑由后端保存时幂等绑定，与老系统 `web/src/api/styles.ts:147` 注释口径一致，前端只查询，行为对齐）。对照老 `poBinding.test.tsx` 的 6 个场景：**缺「绑定查询失败：直接放行不弹框」**（老文件 125 行有，新 4 个 it 未含；实现 `ProductionPage.tsx:983-984` 有 catch 放行分支但无测试覆盖，报告清单 #15 声称覆盖「查询失败」不属实）。
- **测试质量抽查**（新建/保存POST/删除/反审核含BOM/BOM-PO/脱敏共 6+ 条）：全部为行为与载荷断言，无快照堆砌。
- **shell.test.tsx 1s→5s**：合理的时序修复（新增第 5 个测试文件后 worker 抢占 CPU；带注释说明原因；报告给出单跑/串行/基线均过的佐证），非掩盖回归。
- **menuKey="生产制单"**：正确。已核实后端 `MenuCatalog.cs:15`、`ProductionController.cs:17`、老系统 `MENU` 常量均为「生产制单」，显式传参解决了菜单树 label「生产通知单」与权限菜单名不一致的问题。DocToolbar 的 `menuKey` 是既有 prop，骨架只消费未改签名。

## 全局约束（grep 实测）

- em/en dash：diff 与 ProductionPage.tsx 均无（命中项全是 `...` 展开符）✅;emoji 无 ✅
- 图标全部 `@phosphor-icons/react` ✅
- 弹窗四律：三个新 Dialog 均 `bg-white` 不透明白底 ✅；弹窗内无表格，sticky/nowrap 不涉及；页面表格 th 已加 `whitespace-nowrap` ✅
- 老工程 `web/` 与后端 `src/` 零改动 ✅（审查包文件清单仅 web2 五个文件）
- 视觉令牌：主体取自 future.css;**例外** `ProductionPage.tsx:1258` 新徽章用了 `#b45309`（不在 `[data-theme="future"]` 令牌内）,`#9aa5b1` 多处使用亦非令牌色（沿用基线，非本任务新增）

## 发现

- **Important** — `web2/src/__tests__/production.test.tsx`(BOM-PO describe)：缺「po-bindings 查询失败直接放行」用例，老系统 `web/src/__tests__/poBinding.test.tsx:125` 有对应场景；`ProductionPage.tsx:983` 的 catch 分支零覆盖，报告清单 #15 的「查询失败 直接放行」描述与实际测试不符。
- **Minor** — `web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx:1525`：文案用半角标点 `已绑定 PO:...,是否继续使用?`，需求书引文为全角「已绑定 PO：XXX，是否继续使用」。测试自洽，但与需求书字面及老系统全角风格不一致。
- **Minor** — `web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx:1258`:`text-[#b45309]` 硬编码非令牌色；`#9aa5b1` 多处（154/297/341/433/533/664 行）沿用基线非令牌色。
- **Minor** — 「无绑定直接放行」未独立成 it，与「仅绑定当前 PO」合并且合并用例只喂 `[PO-1]`，空数组路径无显式断言。

## ⚠️ 项

- 未审核可编辑表单的 默认单价 input 在无单价权限时仍显示原值（与老系统 `ProductionNoticePage.tsx:777` 行为一致，非偏离；真正防线靠后端剥离）。后续若收紧需前后端一起改。
- 「三级审核」名不副实：数据层单级，报告已如实披露，FlowSteps 四步为展示映射。
- shell.test 超时放宽属合理修复，但建议后续测试文件继续增多时考虑 vitest 并发配置，而非继续抬超时。

## 结论

- **Spec 合规：✅**（必含场景全覆盖，范围外项如实列出；唯一实质性缺口是 BOM-PO 查询失败分支的测试缺失，实现本身正确）
- **代码质量：Approved**（建议补 1 条查询失败用例与文案全角标点，均不阻塞合入）

</details>

# Task 4（采购订单-来料）审查结论

## Spec 合规（对照需求书逐条）

| 条目 | 结论 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|
| 列表查询（单号/供应商/日期） | ✅ | 打开弹窗 keyword 服务端搜索 + page 回 1 有真实断言（test "查询" 断言 `keyword=供应商A&page=1`)。报告称"老页本无日期过滤"**核实属实**：`web/src/pages/production/PurchaseOrderListPage.tsx:19,60` 只有 keyword 状态，日期仅是列 |
| 新建/编辑 | ✅ | 新建 POST、未审核 PUT（"保存修改"断言 PUT body 数量 150/备注"急"/单头生产单号优先） |
| 带入库存数量显示 | ✅ | 可用库存绿色 + tooltip「实时库存已够需求」、已订数量橙色「已下单 5」+ tooltip、默认勾选 5 个纯函数 it 对照 purchaseOrderDrawerStock 规则 |
| **补料带入锁生产单号** | ✅ | 核心场景覆盖齐全：`toBeDisabled()` + `getByTitle(/补料单已挂生产单号,锁定不可改/)` 双断言齐；未挂行 `toBeEnabled()` + 空值 + 手填 "SC-9" 进 payload；保存后 `mark-purchased` 两条路径均断言。对照老 `replenishPoLock.test.tsx:127-158` 两个 PO 场景 1:1（老文件第三个 describe 是塑胶入仓单，不属本任务范围）。tooltip 为需求书要求的新增，合理 |
| 打印契约 | ✅ | 4 个纯函数 it 对照 `printContracts.test.ts:24-73` 采购段逐条对应；7 条注意事项/落款/抬头经人工逐字比对**原样照抄**；hidePrice 脱敏、HTML 转义、货币回落均覆盖 |
| 删除/审核权限位 | ✅ | 无删除/无审核/无单价金额 3 个 it 真实断言不渲染；**menuKey 核实**：前端 `MENU = "采购订单"` 显式传 `menuKey={MENU}`，后端 `MenuCatalog.cs:17` `new("物料管理","采购订单")` + `PurchaseOrderController.cs:17` `Menu = "采购订单"`，三方一致 |
| 全局约束 | ✅ | web2/src 全目录 grep 无 em/en dash、无 emoji；图标全 @phosphor-icons；`var(--warning,#d97706)` 带兜底；骨架组件/hooks 零改动（diff 仅 9 个 web2 文件，web/ 与 src/ErpApi 零触碰） |
| 弹窗四律 | ✅ | PickerDialog：`bg-[#ffffff]` 不透明 + `max-h-[85vh]` + `pickerThCls` 含 `sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white whitespace-nowrap`；编辑/查看网格 th 同样四要素齐 |

测试抽查（6 个，全部真实断言）：首进水合（表单值/徽章/按钮）、录入清单（带出/勾选规则/双 tooltip）、保存 POST body 逐字段、锁定 disabled+tooltip+payload、三级审核链端点+按钮切换、打印 HTML 内容。测试数 3+4+2+4+2+3+5+2+4=29，与报告一致。端点 URL 与后端控制器路由（`/api/purchase-orders`、`/api/replenishments/{no}/mark-purchased`、`待采购` 过滤参数）核对一致。

## 发现

**Critical**：无。

**Important**：无。

**Minor**：
1. `web2/src/pages/PurchaseOrderPage.tsx:172,199`：经理已审徽章引入 `#2563eb`/`#1d4ed8` 蓝色，**不在 future.css `[data-theme="future"]` 令牌中**（其余硬编码 hex 均与令牌值一致，且 ProductionPage 有 58 处同模式先例，唯独此蓝是新色）。建议改用令牌或纳入令牌表。
2. 打印契约测试弱化：老 `printContracts.test.ts:45` 用 `for (const n of PRINT_NOTES)` 断言全 7 条，新测试只断言 2 个片段。注意事项数组本身已人工核对逐字一致，风险低，但契约保护变弱。
3. `PurchaseOrderPage.tsx` buildBody 中 `.filter((r) => r.物料编号 && Number(r.数量) > 0)` 会**静默丢弃**已勾选但数量<=0 的行（仅当全丢时才提示），用户无感知。建议保存前提示被丢弃行。

## ⚠️ 项

- **物料选择器简化（自报偏离，核实属实）**：老 `MaterialPicker.tsx:22,59,63` 确有「只查有库存」复选 + 50/页服务端分页；新版仅 keyword 搜索取前 50。判定**可接受的简化**：加行是次要路径（主路径为录入清单/补料带入），keyword 服务端搜索可定位物料，不至于阻塞使用；但属功能回归，建议后续迭代补上 `有库存` 参数与分页。
- **新旧对照的锁定场景未在真实栈复现**（真实库无符合条件的补料单），仅靠两版测试契约保证；两版断言逐项对应，可接受。
- 报告遗留 4/5（DocToolbar 禁用 tooltip 试点文案、usePerms 走 admin 端点）为 Task 2 遗留，非本任务引入。
- 报告自报的 vitest 63 全过 / tsc / oxlint 结果按审查方法未重跑，未独立验证。

## 质量结论

**通过**。实现忠实对齐老系统：URL/中文字段原样、纯逻辑抽 `lib/purchaseOrder.ts` 与 `lib/printPurchaseOrder.ts` 供页面与测试共用（避免组件文件导出非组件）、权限键经后端 MenuCatalog 实证核对、核心锁定场景测试扎实。3 个 Minor 均不阻塞合并，蓝色令牌与打印契约断言建议在下一迭代顺手修。

# Task 5 审查结论：采购入仓单/采购退仓单（web2）

## Spec 合规（逐条对照需求书 task-5-brief.md Step 1 清单）

- 入仓单新建（送货单号手填必填、供应商、付款方式、仓库默认来料仓、明细加行/整单带入）✅ — 页面 HeaderForm + doSave 校验齐全；测试「保存：送货单号必填校验后 POST」先负后正断言（diff:455-485）
- 入仓单审核 = 入库存 ✅ — 测试断言 POST `/api/purchase-receipts/{单号}/approve` + 状态翻转 + 反审核出现/删除消失（diff:515-531）；后端库存口径实证：`src/ErpApi/Engines/Inventory/MaterialInventoryService.cs:16` 只统计 `审核='1'` 的入仓明细，业务链闭环
- 退仓单 = 退回供应商（审核即扣库存）✅ — 测试断言 POST `/api/purchase-returns/{单号}/approve`（diff:615-629）；后端实证 `MaterialInventoryService.cs:27` 审核退仓扣库存；表头含入仓单号、无送货单号、电脑单号后端生成（diff:566-579）
- 入仓/退仓查询 ✅ — 双页签 + 上月/本月/下月 + 日期区间 + 审核情况/物料类别/关键字过滤下发均有真实 URL 断言（diff:656-699）；明细/汇总子页签、导出 CSV、打印、双击回单据页签开整单均覆盖（入仓双击取入库单号、退仓取单号，与后端 DTO 注释一致）
- 关联采购单号显示 ✅ — 查看态明细 订单单号 列（diff:2073）、入仓查询明细 订单单号 列、整单带入 payload `订单单号=采购单号`（diff:480）、纯函数 orderRowToLine 断言（diff:793-804）
- 补料口径（数量只入库存、不强制关联生产通知单）✅ — 测试「手选物料加行」真实断言 payload `订单单号/生产单号` 均 undefined（diff:507-508）；编辑网格顶部有口径明示文案（diff:1897-1899）

## 全局约束核查

- 无 em/en dash ✅（grep web2/src 零命中）；无 emoji ✅；图标全部 @phosphor-icons/react ✅
- 视觉令牌 ✅ — 页面硬编码 hex（#16a34a/#15803d/#1a2330/#5f6b7d 等）逐值等于 `future.css:15-55` 的令牌值，且与已过审的 Task 4 `PurchaseOrderPage.tsx` 模式完全一致（portal 逃逸 theme 作用域的既定写法，`future.css:94` 有注释说明）；portal 内未见无兜底的裸 `var(--f-*)`
- 弹窗四律 ✅ — PickerDialog：`bg-[#ffffff]` 不透明 + `max-h-[85vh]`；`pickerThCls`/`qThCls` 均 `sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white whitespace-nowrap`；删除确认 Dialog `bg-white`；OpenDocDialog 为 Task 2 骨架未改动
- 骨架组件/hooks 只消费 ✅ — diff 9 个文件无 components/hooks 改动；DocToolbar/usePerms/FlowSteps 等签名未动
- 老工程 web/ 与后端零改动 ✅（diff 文件清单仅 web2）
- 权限键实证 ✅ — `src/ErpApi/Features/Admin/MenuCatalog.cs:17` 物料管理组含「采购入仓单」「采购退仓单」，无「采购出仓单」；控制器 Menu 常量：`PurchaseReceiptController.cs:18`="采购入仓单"、`PurchaseReturnController.cs:18`="采购退仓单"；页面 `kind.menu` 与之逐字一致

## 测试真实性抽查（5/22）

1. 审核=入库存（diff:515）：断言 approve URL + UI 状态翻转 + 删除按钮消失，非空断言 ✅
2. 退仓审核（diff:615）：断言退仓 approve 端点 + 已审核徽章 ✅
3. 补料口径（diff:487）：payload 字段级 undefined 断言 ✅
4. 整单带入（diff:428）：分组合并（130=80+50）、带入 2 行、已完成×2、改量后「欠 30」实时状态 ✅
5. 查询过滤下发（diff:656）：decodeURIComponent 后断言 `审核情况=已审核`/`物料类别=面料`/`keyword=` 三段下发 + 双击 GET 整单 ✅
6. 权限新名反向锁定（diff:566 + 778）：仅授权「采购退仓单」时退仓页审核/删除可见；无审核位时审核按钮不渲染而删除在 — 若代码误用旧名 can() 必为 false，测试会红，锁定有效 ✅

与老 `web/src/__tests__/materialDocs.test.ts` 比对：老文件仅 6 例纯函数测试（合计/validLines/生产制单回填/复制单预填），无页面级场景；web2 新套件场景覆盖远超老版，无场景丢失（老文件的复制单/生产制单回填属领料/退料单功能，不在本任务口径）。

实测验证：单跑新测试文件 `npx vitest run src/__tests__/purchaseReceipt.test.tsx` → **22/22 通过**（2.12s）。报告自报的 tsc/oxlint/全量回归未重跑（按审查方法约定）。

## 发现

- Critical：无
- Important：无
- Minor 1：测试归组错位 —「退仓查询」用例放在 `describe("入仓查询")` 内（`web2/src/__tests__/purchaseReceipt.test.tsx`，diff:701 起），仅影响报告输出可读性
- Minor 2：首进自动开单在组件渲染体内 `set单号(firstNo)`（diff:2290-2293），属 React 合法 derived-state 写法但非常规；同 Task 4 模式，不改
- Minor 3：单文件 1993 行，与 PurchaseOrderPage 同量级，可接受但后续任务宜留意拆分阈值

## ⚠️ 项（均可 deferred，不阻塞通过）

- 批量审核未移植：需求书清单未列，单张审核已覆盖且后端 approve 为单级；建议在 backlog 登记，后续按需补
- 退仓单库内无真实数据：查看态/查询仅桩测试 + playwright 空态对照；后端 DTO/库存扣减已实证，可接受，待有真实退仓单后补一次新旧对照截图

## 质量结论

**通过（PASS）**。需求书 Step 1 五条清单全部落地且有真实断言；权限键经 MenuCatalog/控制器双重实证并用权限反向测试锁定；全局约束（dash/emoji/图标/令牌/弹窗四律/骨架只消费/web+后端零改动）逐项核实无违例；22 例测试抽查 6 例均为行为级断言且单文件实测全绿。代码风格与已过审的 Task 4 页面同构，纯函数层（purchaseReceipt.ts/tableExport.ts/printMaterialDoc.ts）抽取合理、注释标明老系统出处。仅 3 个 Minor（测试归组、渲染内 setState 写法、文件体量），均不阻塞合入。

## Spec 合规（逐条）

## 重点核查结果

- **三级链测试真实性**：✅ `plasticIssue.test.tsx`「审核流转」两例真实断言精确端点（`/supervisor-approve`、`/manager-approve`、`/approve`、`/unapprove` 全 URL 匹配）+ 按钮逐级出现/消失 + 「已审核」状态翻转 + 删除按钮消现，非空转。
- **28 个新测试抽查**：实际通读全部 28 例（2 打开 + 4 批量领料 + 2 手选校验 + 2 审核 + 2 查询 + 1 打印 + 4 权限 + 11 纯函数），抽查的 5+ 例（多单调入的 `档=塑胶&按货号=true` 双参数断言、确定带入的空白行替换与数量 10/7 顺序、三级链、保存三段拦截不发 POST、权限位 4 例）均为实质断言。
- **测试运行状态**：按审查方法未重跑；「113 全过 / tsc 0 错 / oxlint 无新增 / 老 web 424 全过」为报告自报，未独立验证。

## 发现

**Critical**：无。
**Important**：无。

**Minor**：
1. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:439` LinesView 查看态比老查看态多出 规格/仓位号/备注 三列（老=同 11 列禁用输入框）。信息超集、无害，但属保真偏差，报告未自报。
2. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:1324`、`:1386` 物料/生产制单选择弹窗无加载态（basis 弹窗有骨架，这两个只有「无匹配」空态），加载期间表格空白。
3. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:431` 数量合计 `sumQty(rows)` 无千分位（老 antd Statistic 默认分组显示），大数可读性略差。
4. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:548` `ColumnDef<PlasticIssueHeader, any>[]` 用了 `any`（oxlint 未报错，与项目现状兼容，仅记录）。

## ⚠️ 项（报告自报的 deferred 判定）

- **批量审核未移植**（老 `PlasticIssueFormPage.tsx:101-116`、`:270`，依附内嵌历史列表 rowSelection）：**可 deferred**。OpenDocDialog 取代内嵌列表是 Task 5 起已确立的架构口径，批量审核失去载体属架构替代的连带结果；但这是相对老系统的真实功能缺口，建议登记跟进项（在 OpenDocDialog 加多选审核）。
- **打印合并表格未移植**（老 `:208`）：**可接受**。该勾选项只在老「整页 window.print」口径下有意义，新窗口单据打印口径下无对应物。
- **「重量合计 0.0」占位未保留**（老 `:263`，`value={"0.0"}` 写死）：**不保留是正确判断**，该占位零信息量，保留反而是保真迷信。
- **批量领料弹窗行为超出老塑胶页**：老塑胶页「按生产单带入」仅为单档 Input.Search（`:277-279`，不带 按货号），多单合并+按货号=true+货号筛选在老系统只属于 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:274-313`（来料领料）。新版把该能力移植到塑胶页，符合需求书「issueBasisPick 场景逐条」的字面要求（spec ✅），但属行为增强，建议与产品确认塑胶侧统一为此口径。代码本身是对 MaterialLineTable 逻辑的忠实照抄（含 `prev.filter(l => l.物料编号)` 空白行替换语义），质量无问题。
- **单文件行数**：`PlasticIssuePage.tsx` 1565 行，处于兄弟页区间（ProductionPage 1551 / PurchaseOrderPage 1726 / PurchaseReceiptPage 1993）之内，**未失控**，但为项目高位；后续可把三个 PickerDialog 抽出，非本次阻断项。

## 质量结论

**通过**。Spec 条目全部 ✅，无 Critical/Important；全局约束（dash/emoji/图标/令牌/弹窗四律/骨架零改动/老工程与后端零改动/menuKey 实证）逐项 grep 实证通过；三级链与 issueBasisPick 两条核心契约的测试均为真实断言且与老系统逐字对照一致。遗留为 4 个 Minor 与「批量审核」一个建议跟进的 deferred 缺口。唯一未能独立验证的是测试全绿（按要求未重跑，采信报告自报）。

# Task 7（塑胶入仓单）审查结论

## Spec 合规（对照需求书逐条）

- ✅ **入仓新建**：送货单号/供应商/仓库必填，逐级拦截（送货单号 → 供应商 → 仓库 → 有效明细），POST 载荷对照后端 DTO。测试「保存」2 例真实断言（含 POST body `toMatchObject`、空串字段不落、锁定行带生产单号）。
- ✅ **审核（=入仓）/反审核/删除**：单级流转，已审核无删除/审核按钮。后端契约实证 `PlasticReceiptController.cs:63-96`：approve 可返回 `{提示,警告}`、无则 204；web2 侧 `api()` 对 204 返回 undefined（`web2/src/lib/api.ts:27`），`{提示}` 优先展示有专测。
- ✅ **`?单号=` 直开**：**真实测试覆盖**（`plasticReceipt.test.tsx:219-228`）——把首进列表置空，断言 `GET /api/plastic-receipts/SR20260915001` 直发且不依赖最新单。注：老 `PlasticReceiptFormPage` 本身无此参数，`?单号=` 在老系统属塑胶页族能力（`PlasticProcessPurchaseOrderPage.tsx:115` 等），需求书口径为"试点已承诺的塑胶页能力"，新版补齐算履约。
- ✅ **喷油改单自动更新展示**：同步在后端（`SprayPlanReceiptSyncService.cs:107-118` 实证：已审核不动、未审核按映射更新）。前端两点落实：打开弹窗点行强制 `invalidateQueries` 详情、审核/反审核后 `refetch`。测试（`:537-559`）模拟后台 30→55 改单后重开，断言新 GET 发出、55 出现、30 消失，真实有效。
- ✅ **补料入塑胶锁生产单号**：与老系统 `PlasticReceiptFormPage.tsx:123-125` 的 `锁定生产单号: r.生产单号 ? true : undefined` 逐字同口径；老 `LineTable.tsx:51-53` 锁定行 disabled 只读。新版 disabled + `title` tooltip（tooltip 是增强，老系统无，不超口径）；未挂生产单号的行维持可编辑有断言。保存 payload 带锁定行生产单号有断言。
- ✅ **塑胶退仓单入口**：`menu.ts` 占位卡片（置灰），名字与老 `web/src/nav/menuTree.tsx:148` 一致（退仓=plastic-warehouse-returns，与塑胶退料单是两单）。
- ✅ **路由 `/plastic-receipts`**：MainLayout PAGES/PAGE_LOADERS 注册。

## 全局约束核查

- ✅ 无 em/en dash、无 emoji：node 扫 8 个改动文件全量，0 命中（报告所称 endpoints.ts "箭头"为 ASCII `->`，无碍）。
- ✅ 图标全部 `@phosphor-icons/react`。
- ✅ 弹窗四律：PickerDialog `bg-[#ffffff]` 不透明 + `max-h-[85vh]`；th `sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white whitespace-nowrap`；编辑/查看网格同规。
- ✅ 骨架组件/hooks 零改动（diff 8 文件不含 components/hooks）；`web/` 与 `src/ErpApi` 零改动。
- ✅ menuKey 实证：`MenuCatalog.cs:48` `("塑胶仓储","塑胶入仓单")` + 控制器 `Menu = "塑胶入仓单"`；DocToolbar 一律显式 `menuKey={MENU}`，测试 perms 行显式锁定该键。
- ⚠️ 视觉令牌：页面用字面 hex（`#1a2330`/`#15803d` 等），均为 `future.css` 令牌同值，且与 Task 5/6 页面同范式（PlasticIssuePage 93 处同款），判合规但不算严格 var() 消费——全工程既有做法，非本任务新增问题。

## 测试抽查（5+ 真实断言）

1. `?单号=` 直开：断言 GET detail URL 直发 ✅
2. 带入锁行：`toBeDisabled` + `title` tooltip 文案 + 未锁行 `not.toBeDisabled` ✅
3. 保存 POST body：`toMatchObject` 单头五字段 + 明细 `单价 toBeUndefined`、未锁行 `生产单号 toBeUndefined` ✅
4. 审核后详情 GET 次数 `>=2`（refetch 时机）+ 按钮组切换 ✅
5. 喷油改单 30→55：`queryByText("30")).not.toBeInTheDocument()` 旧值消失 ✅
6. 无单价位：查看态 `***`、编辑网格无单价列、打印 HTML 不含 `<th>金额</th>` ✅

亲跑验证：`vitest run src/__tests__/plasticReceipt.test.tsx` **27/27 通过**；`tsc -b` **exit 0**（按要求未重跑全套与老系统回归）。

## 发现

**Critical：无。**

**Important（1 项）：**
- 塑胶入仓查询页签未移植，造成**同模块一致性缺口**：Task 5（PurchaseReceiptPage QueryPanel，采购入仓/退仓双页签）与 Task 6（PlasticIssueQueryPanel）都已做"单据+查询"双页签，老系统同路由也有「塑胶入仓查询」（`web/src/App.tsx:274` DocQueryTabs），后端端点与权限键俱在（`MenuCatalog.cs:65` 塑胶报表/塑胶入仓查询、`PlasticReceiptQueryController.cs:15`）。需求书确实未列，不算 spec 违约，但 Task 7 落地后塑胶入仓单成了 web2 里唯一没有查询页签的入仓类单据，用户丧失对账用的明细/汇总查询。**建议立为后续任务**（有现成 QueryPanel 范式，成本低），不宜静默 deferred。报告已在 Concerns 自报。

**Minor（2 项）：**
- `?单号=` 消费后参数保留在 URL（`PlasticReceiptPage.tsx:786-793`）：dialog 换单后浏览器刷新会跳回参数单；且 keep-alive 下 `autoNoDone` ref 持久，同实例第二次带不同 `?单号=` 进入不会重开。`?ppo=` 已清参数，两者口径不一。当前 web2 内暂无 `?单号=` 跳转方，影响有限。
- `act()` 在审核同时返回 `{提示}+{警告}` 时 toast tone="err"（diff 行 2131），但审核实际已成功，语义偏误导；建议此组合用 ok 或中立 tone。

**⚠️ 项：**
- 报告的 playwright 新旧对照（截图/JSON 在 `shots/`，脚本未入库）我未逐图核验；老系统 83 文件 424 测试回归与 oxlint 基线未重跑（按审查指示），报告自报均过。

## 质量结论

**通过（有条件）**。代码质量良好：纯逻辑正确抽离到 `lib/plasticReceipt.ts` 且单测覆盖，权限位/脱敏/打印 hidePrice 链路完整，API 层 URL 与中文字段照抄老系统并经后端控制器实证，27 个测试断言均为真实行为断言无凑数。Spec 必含场景全 ✅。唯一需要决策的是塑胶入仓查询页签：按 Task 5/6 已建立的双页签惯例，建议补一个后续小任务移植，不作为本任务验收阻塞。

- 无死循环：清参后 effect 重触发，`if (!no && !ppo) return` 早退；keep-alive 下同实例再次带参导航时 searchParams 变化可重新触发。
- 测试真实：LocationProbe 断言消费后 `location.search === ""`（test :247-248）。

**3. (Minor) {提示}+{警告} toast tone — ADDRESSED**

- `PlasticReceiptPage.tsx:862` 组合时 `tone: "err"` 改 `"ok"`；DocToast ok = `text-[#15803d]`（`DocToast.tsx:9-11`）。
- 测试断言 toast className 含 `text-[#15803d]` 且不含 `text-[#dc2626]`（test :283-285），为真实 DOM 断言。

## 新破坏

无 Critical/Important。一条低severity 观察（不构成裁决项）：keep-alive 下若用户停在查询页签时同实例收到 `?单号=` 导航，effect 会加载该单但不切回 doc 页签（`PlasticReceiptPage.tsx:789-801` 未 setTab("doc")），单据在后台页签加载；双击链路无此问题（onOpenDoc 显式 setTab)。app 内无 ?单号= 内部链接，仅外部直开/刷新场景（均为新挂载，tab 默认 doc)，实际触发面极小。

## 三、重点裁决：分次出库抽屉未移植

**老系统实证**:`MaterialDocPage.tsx:93-103` 对 material-issues **故意不暴露整单「审核」入口**，出库唯一路径就是 `MaterialIssueOutboundDrawer`(POST /outbound，按行「本次出库」可部分、可跳行，全部出完自动置已审核）。即该抽屉在老 UI 是**主流程**，不是旁路。

**裁决：Important，不升级 Critical。** 理由：

1. 需求书 Step 1 明写「审核（=出库）」，且括号注明「与塑胶领料单行为对齐（用户明确要求两边仓口径一致）」——塑胶侧（Task 6）本就无分次出库，本任务实现的就是被点名的口径；
2. 后端 /approve 是一等公民端点，库存台账按「已出数量」实时聚合（`MaterialInventoryService.cs:13`)，与分次出库共用口径，不存在数据口径断裂；
3. 但能力缺口是真实的：老流程支持「先出一部分、剩余再出」，web2 只能整单出。若装配部实际存在分批领料，需老系统兜底或另立任务。报告已在 Concerns 自报，未隐瞒。

**建议**：作为后续任务单独立项（或在迁移切换前由业务确认「来料仓不需要分次出库」)，不阻塞本任务验收。

## 四、测试抽查（26 条，grep 计数=26，与报告一致）

抽查 7 条均真实断言、无套套逻辑：

- 三级链（materialIssue.test.tsx):assert 精确端点 URL `supervisor-approve`→`manager-approve`→`approve`、状态文案逐级迁移、审核后删除入口消失。库存扣减属后端职责，前端测到「端点+refetch 后状态」为止，粒度合理
- 多单调入：assert 2 次 issue-basis 请求、URL 含 `档=来料`+`按货号=true`、合并后 HH-A 在 HH-B 前、默认全选、货号筛选 options=["","HH-A","HH-B"]
- 半成品仓档：assert `档=半成品`、**不**带 `按货号`、材料列补「半成品」
- 保存成功：assert POST 载荷字段（部门默认装配部/仓库来料仓/明细数量转数值/备注去空白）
- 权限位 4 条：保存/审核/删除/反审核/打印/打开位各自门控，含「无打开位查询页签整页无权」
- 查询过滤：assert `审核情况=`/`物料类别=`/`keyword=` 下发 + 双击行 GET 整单回单据页签
- 打印：有/无「单价」位两例，价格列出现与消失都断言

## 五、发现清单

**Critical**：无。

**Important**:
1. 分次出库抽屉（POST /outbound 部分出库）未移植 — 老 UI 主流程出库路径，见上裁决。出处：`web/src/pages/materials/MaterialIssueOutboundDrawer.tsx:9`、`web/src/pages/materials/MaterialDocPage.tsx:103`；新侧对应位置 `web2/src/pages/MaterialIssuePage.tsx:1046`（仅整单 approve)。

**Minor**:
1. 删除按钮对「未审核但有已出数量」的单仍会渲染（后端 `MaterialIssueService.cs:128` 会 Conflict 拦截并回消息），前端只靠 toast 兜底。老系统同样如此（口径未变），不算回归，仅记录。`web2/src/pages/MaterialIssuePage.tsx` 删除 action 段。
2. 下推 `?basis=` 的档取自初始表单闭包（默认来料仓）并带 `eslint-disable exhaustive-deps`(`MaterialIssuePage.tsx:891` 附近）；行为与报告自述一致（档随默认来料仓），可用但脆弱，仓库默认策略变更时会静默跟随。

**⚠️ 项**:
- 报告自报的另两项缺口（半成品/成品「入仓单带入」、批量审核/复制单）经抽查属实：老 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:103-157` 确有入仓单带入，新侧未做；均在必含场景之外，与 Task 5/6/7 同范式，记录即可。
- 查询页签与单据共用权限键与塑胶侧分离模式不同，属后端实证差异，报告与代码注释都已标注，无误导。
- 报告声称的 playwright 截图/新旧对照数据不在审查包内，本轮未复核该证据本身（对照 JSON 字段级 match 仅采信报告自述）。

## 六、质量结论

**通过（附 1 项 Important 跟踪项）**。实现保真度高：档映射、状态文案、说明文案逐字对照老代码；依据挑选纯函数与塑胶侧同源复用，两仓口径天然一致；权限键实证扎实；测试是真断言而非摆设。分次出库缺口由 spec 的「审核=出库+两边仓一致」豁免，但因它是老系统主流程出库路径，必须在迁移收口前由业务确认或另立任务，建议写进批次级 TODO。

</details>

The old system's main flow was partial outbound. This is a real capability gap that needs user/business confirmation. Options: (a) park with ruling and surface to user at the end; (b) add a task to port the partial-outbound drawer. Given the user explicitly said "领料单审核=出库" as the business rule earlier （业务口径已和用户确认）, and the new flow covers it, I rule: defer to end-of-batch user confirmation — it's a business decision whether partial outbound is still needed. Record ruling in ledger, mark task complete with the Important parked as a business-confirmation item (not a code defect).

Per skill rules: Important findings enter fix loop unless ruled. The reviewer itself says "由 spec 的「审核=出库+两边仓一致」豁免" and recommends batch-level TODO + business confirmation. Since spec/plan mandates this口径， this is a plan-vs-finding conflict → my ruling. Ruling: 保持需求书口径（审核=整单出库），分次出库列入批次收尾时向用户确认；若用户需要，另立任务移植。That's consistent.

Ledger + dispatch Task 9.

| 必含场景对照旧测试 | ✅ | 旧测试 4 场景（过滤+保存、旧单还原、塑胶 POST+跳转、装配 POST+跳转）全部对齐，新版另补校验拦截/审核链/删除/打印/权限/纯函数/打印契约，超集覆盖 |

## 全局约束核查（grep 实证）

- 无 em/en dash、无 emoji：✅（对 web2/src 全量 grep 零命中）
- 图标全 @phosphor-icons：✅（两个新文件仅 import `@phosphor-icons/react`）
- 视觉令牌：✅ 硬编码 hex（#1a2330/#ffffff/#d97706）与 `future.css` [data-theme="future"] 令牌值一一对应，且与 Task 7 `PlasticIssuePage.tsx:615` 等既有页面写法逐字一致；`var(--warning,#d97706)` 带兜底
- 弹窗四律：✅ 不透明白底 `bg-[#ffffff]`、`max-h-[85vh]`、sticky th `sticky top-0 z-10 ... bg-white`、`whitespace-nowrap`，全部弹层（PickerDialog/下加工单/删除确认/表格）一致
- 骨架组件/hooks 只消费不改签名：✅ 改动清单无 components/ 或 hooks/ 文件
- web/ 与后端零改动：✅ diff 12 文件全在 web2/
- menuKey 实证+显式传：✅ `MenuCatalog.cs:12` 有（基础资料，款号资料）、`:21` 有（发外加工，装配加工采购单）；`StyleController.cs:16` 页面门常量吻合；四处 DocToolbar 均显式 `menuKey={DOC_MENU}`
- 后端端点实证：✅ `PlasticProcessPurchaseOrderController.cs:10` 存在 `api/plastic-process-purchase-orders`

## 测试抽查（22 个中查 10 个，全为真实断言）

1. 行级客户过滤：断言 datalist option 集合含/不含（非仅渲染）✅
2. 保存 POST：断言 `生产明细[0..1]` 逐行 客户编号/客户名称/装配方式 + 物料明细 `[款号,物料编号,需求数量]` 全等 ✅
3. 校验拦截：断言提示文案且**未发** POST ✅
4. 旧单还原：快照 999 != BOM 20 实证不重算 ✅
5. 塑胶 POST：断言 加工厂编号/客户名称/明细行（加工内容=啤塑、数量=20、单价=0.5）✅
6. 逐级校验：缺加工厂→缺加工内容两步拦截且全程无 POST ✅
7. 装配 POST：单头供应商=加工厂+生产明细一行带行级字段+跳回 `?单号=` 直开新单 ✅
8. 三级审核链：按钮按位切换+徽章文案 ✅
9. `collectProductionLines` 纯函数：同下标优先+按货号回落+空行过滤 ✅
10. 打印契约：含 10 行补足计数（`<tr>` 总数=16）与克重聚合 KG 换算 ✅

## 发现

### Important

1. **下加工单跳塑胶加工采购单：静默断链且成功提示不可见**（`web2/src/pages/AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:744`）。`navigate("/plastic-process-purchase-orders?单号=...")` 落入未注册路由，`MainLayout.tsx:138` 的 `showHome = !PAGES[path]` 使其渲染宫格首页；更糟的是成功 toast（含新单号 SJ-NEW）设在被 keep-alive `hidden` 的本页上（`MainLayout.tsx:171`），用户点"确定"后看到的就是首页，**没有任何确认、单号也拿不到**——比"跳错页"更差，是"操作结果对用户的彻底丢失"。POST 本身成功、数据无损，故不升 Critical。
   **裁决建议**：不接受"等后续批次页面落地即通"的现状，本批就应修。推荐最小修法：跳转前判断目标路由是否已注册，未注册则不跳，留在本页并把 toast 改为"塑胶加工采购单 SJ-NEW 已创建（该单据页后续批次上线，请记下单号）"。不推荐跳老系统深链：现有"回旧版"先例（`TopBar.tsx:128`）是硬编码 `localhost:5173` 的顶层链接，深链会把 web2 页面逻辑耦合到 dev 地址，比留页提示更重。

### Minor

2. "行级空→单头"后端回落路径无直接断言：`collectProductionLines` 测试覆盖了同下标优先与按货号匹配，但没有"完全无匹配产品行 → 客户字段 undefined 交由后端回落单头"的用例。实现正确（照抄旧页口径），仅测试缺口。
3. 需求书文件名 `web2/src/nav/futureMenu.ts` 与实际 `web2/src/nav/menu.ts` 不符（nav/ 下只有 menu.ts），属需求书过时，实现改对了地方。
4. 页面拆成 3 文件（Page/Header/Tables）超出需求书 Files 清单，但 1548 行单文件拆分有先例且合理，不算违规。

### ⚠️ 项（不阻塞）

- 生产制单选择器 onProductionPick 无单测（报告自报，交互模式同 Task 6/7 已验证，可接受）。
- datalist 替代 antd 搜索下拉，无高亮匹配项，交互略简（报告自报，jsdom 可测性换来的合理取舍）。
- usePerms 试点 admin 端点为 Task 2 遗留，非本任务范围。
- 旧测试还断言了 `navigate` 的 URL 参数本身，新测试塑胶路径无法断言落点（页面不存在），与 Important 1 同根。

## 质量结论

实现质量高：纯逻辑抽到 `lib/assemblyPurchase.ts` 页面与测试共用，行为逐条照抄旧页并带注释标注出处；测试为真实断言非渲染冒烟；约束项全部 grep 实证通过；menuKey 实证链路完整。**有条件通过**：Important 1（跳转断链+toast 不可见）建议在本批修掉（一行路由存在性判断+toast 文案），Minor 2 的回落测试用例可顺手补上；其余 ⚠️ 项均有报告自报与先例支撑，不阻塞合入。

</details>

复核完成。逐项裁决如下：

## 发现 1(Important)：下加工单跳塑胶加工采购单断链+toast 不可见 — **ADDRESSED**

- **路由注册判断**:`AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:744-756`，塑胶分支先取 `target = "/plastic-process-purchase-orders"`,`MENU_PATHS.has(target)` 为 false 时不 `navigate`，留在本页并 `setToast({ text: 塑胶加工采购单 ${created} 已创建(该单据页后续批次上线,请记下单号) })`；装配分支（`:758-760`）逻辑未动，仍跳 `/assembly-purchases?单号=` 直开。修法与裁决要求逐条吻合。
- **MENU_PATHS 真实性**:`web2/src/nav/menu.ts:118-120`,`MENU_PATHS` 不是硬编码清单，而是从 `MENU_TREE` 有 path 的叶子动态 flatMap 得出。与 `MainLayout.tsx:22-33` 的 `PAGES` 对照：MENU_TREE 唯一路径共 10 条（`/production`、`/plastic-issues`、`/assembly-purchases`、`/purchase-orders`、`/purchase-receipts`、`/material-issues`、`/purchase-returns`、`/material-inventory`、`/plastic-receipts`、`/accounts`),PAGES 键同样 10 条，**集合完全相等，当前无漂移**;`/plastic-process-purchase-orders` 确实不在其中，判断结果正确。残余隐患（非本次破坏）:MENU_PATHS 与 PAGES 是两个并列数据源，靠注释（menu.ts:115）约定同步维护，未来加页面时漏改任一侧仍会漂移——建议后续批次考虑从 PAGES 单侧导出，本轮可放行。
- **toast 可见性**:`AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:1555` 在本页内渲染 `<DocToast>`，不跳走即页面不被 keep-alive 隐藏，toast 真实可见；文案含新单号 `created`，符合要求。
- **测试真实性**:`assemblyPurchase.test.tsx:397-403`,POST 断言在前（387-396),toast 断言用全文案正则 `/塑胶加工采购单 SJ-NEW 已创建\(该单据页后续批次上线,请记下单号\)/`——旧代码 toast 无后缀、必然失配，**这是对修复的真实回归断言**(SJ-NEW 来自 fetch 桩 149-150 行）。一点诚实保留：`renderWithProviders`(test/setup.tsx:7-16）用裸 `MemoryRouter` 无 `<Routes>`，即使发生 navigate 页面也不会卸载，所以 `:403` 的「装配加工采购单（新建）」对「不跳走」是弱断言（在 harness 下恒真）；真正的鉴别力在 toast 文案差异。属于 Minor 级测试强度问题，不影响修复正确性。

## 发现 2(Minor):collectProductionLines 回落用例 — **ADDRESSED**

- 新用例 `assemblyPurchase.test.tsx:607-621`：同下标产品行 `{key:11}` 仅有 key 无内容（触发 `assemblyPurchase.ts:216` 的 `pl.产品货号 || pl.客户编号` 判空为 false)，货号 `KX` 与 K1/K2 均不匹配（`:218` find 落空）,`row` 为 undefined → `:228-231` 四字段 undefined。断言 `客户编号/客户名称/装配方式/备注` 逐条 `toBeUndefined`，与实现逐行对应，真实断言。修正后的用例组合（同下标空行+货号不匹配）正确避开了首版踩到的同下标优先规则。

## diff 内新破坏扫描

三个文件改动均为纯增量：menu.ts 仅追加 export;AssemblyPurchasePage 仅在塑胶分支加 if/else,`setPoFor(null)` 位置不变，装配分支逐字未动；测试文件只加断言。未发现 Critical/Important 级新破坏。

## 总结论

**两项均 ADDRESSED，可放行。** 附带两条非阻塞观察（不属本 diff 破坏）:① MENU_PATHS 与 MainLayout PAGES 双源同步靠注释约束，建议页面落地批次收敛为单源；② 「不跳走」断言在当前裸 MemoryRouter harness 下强度有限，真正锚定修复的是 toast 全文案断言，如后续要严格断导航可加 useNavigate spy。

agent_id: agent-107

status: completed

[summary]
复核完成，逐项裁决如下。

## 逐项裁决

**1. 数据源切换 + 响应形状适配 — ADDRESSED**
- 后端实证：`AuthController.cs:26-31` `GET /auth/me/permissions` 返回 `perm.GetByUserAsync(name)`，即 `PermissionService.cs:7-27` 直读 `userbqrpower` 组装的 `IReadOnlyDictionary<string, PermissionFlags>`；`PermissionFlags.cs:7-15` 的 9 个属性就是中文位名（打开/保存/删除/打印/单价/金额/审核/反审核/功能）。JSON 形状 = `{菜单: {打开..功能}}`，与 web2 新增 `MyPermMap`（types.ts）完全吻合，也与旧系统 `web/src/auth/permissions.ts:5` 的 `PermMap` 同构；旧系统 `PermissionContext.tsx:11` 用的正是此端点。
- 适配层确实只在 hook 内：`usePerms.ts` queryFn 换为 `authApi.myPermissions()`，`can()` 由数组 find 改为 `!!map[menuKey]?.[bit]`。`userPermApi` 仍保留在 `endpoints.ts:532` 供 AccountsPage 使用，符合"admin 端点只供账号管理页"的注释口径。

**2. 放行/严格/缺失拒绝语义 — ADDRESSED**
`usePerms.ts:23-27`：`!bit → true`（不变）；`!map → true`（未登录/加载中/失败的放行分支不变，queryFn 失败时 `q.data` 为 undefined）；就位后 `map[menuKey]?.[bit]` 缺失菜单 → undefined → false（拒绝），与原 `find` 后 `!row → false` 等价。

**3. 9 个测试桩切换、断言不变 — ADDRESSED**
diff 触及的测试文件正好 9 个（assemblyPurchase、docSkeleton、materialIssue、plasticIssue、plasticReceipt、production、purchaseOrder、purchaseReceipt、scheduling）。每个文件的 hunk 只改两行：import 加 `permRowsToMap`，桩路由 `endsWith("/perms")` → `endsWith("/me/permissions")`（docSkeleton 改判 `/api/auth/me/permissions`）。perms 字面量与全部行为断言零改动。`setup.tsx` 的 `permRowsToMap` 丢弃 `组`、以 `菜单` 为 key，正确映射新形状。

**4. 两处 Minor — ADDRESSED**
- `ScheduleProductionDialog.tsx` toast tone `err → ok`（diff 确认，仅提示性文案）。
- `ScheduleImportDialog.tsx:295` `#92610a` → `text-[var(--warning,#d97706)]`；令牌定义于 `web2/src/future.css:40`，且同写法已在 PurchaseOrderPage/AssemblyPurchaseHeader/ProductionPage 多处使用，风格一致。

**页面零改动核实**：diff 文件清单中除两个对话框外无任何页面文件；grep 确认 13 处 `const { can } = usePerms()` 调用点均未在 diff 中。

## 新破坏

未发现 Critical/Important 级新破坏。一个无足轻重的观察：queryKey `["user-perms", user]` 未随数据源改名，但 TanStack Query 缓存纯内存无持久化，旧形状缓存不会跨会话残留，不构成问题。

## 报告事实错误（一处，重要）

`perm-source-report.md:29` 声称：**"旧系统 web/ 没有账号权限管理页（grep -rln "admin/accounts" web/src/pages/ 无命中），从不调用该 PUT……该隐患是 web2 新增页独有的"** —— 此说法不属实。

- `web/src/pages/admin/AccountPage.tsx` 存在且就是账号权限管理页：其 `PermDrawer`（第 144-179 行）调用 `userPermApi.get` / `userPermApi.save`（第 154、175 行）。
- grep 无命中是因为 URL 字面量封装在 `web/src/api/admin.ts:47-52`（`/admin/accounts/{user}/perms` 的 GET/PUT），不在 pages/ 目录——报告的 grep 范围选错了。
- 推论修正：PUT 整组替换会清掉非 MenuCatalog 行（如「生产排期」）的隐患**旧系统同样存在**，并非 web2 独有；报告基于错误前提得出的"不存在可对齐的旧行为"也不成立（旧行为就是同样会清行）。不过最终结论"纯前端无法加保护、需后端改动、超出本次约束"依然成立，不受影响。

## 总结论

**通过（PASS）**。修复本身四项裁决要求全部 ADDRESSED，无新破坏。报告的测试/验证结论可信，但其 accounts 章节的前提事实错误（旧系统有 AccountPage.tsx 且同样调用整组替换 PUT）需更正：该隐患是新旧系统共有的后端语义问题，建议在遗留记录中改写，避免后续裁决基于"web2 独有"的错误定性。

</details>

# Task 11（消息中心页）审查结论

## Spec 合规（对照 task-11-brief.md 逐条）

| 条目 | 结论 |
| --- | --- |
| 新建 `web2/src/pages/MessagesPage.tsx` | ✅ 337 行，future 主题（f-panel/f-btn/f-label/f-mono) |
| api 补分页/筛选，不改签名 | ✅ `endpoints.ts:344-355` 签名 `(page,size,onlyUnread)` 未动，仅加 camelCase id → ID 归一化，与 `web/src/api/messages.ts:24` 逐字对应 |
| `nav/menu.ts` + MainLayout 注册 `/messages` | ✅ menu.ts g-base 组加叶子，MainLayout PAGES/PAGE_LOADERS 同步，MENU_PATHS 自动含新路径 |
| 铃铛弹层底部「查看全部」跳本页 | ✅ `MessageBell.tsx` 底部按钮改为 navigate("/messages")，「只看未读」挪头部 |
| 测试 `messages.test.tsx` | ✅ 8 用例，单跑 8/8 通过（vitest run 实测） |
| 列表分页/只看未读/全部 | ✅ 用例 1/2/3 真实断言 query 参数（onlyUnread/page/size）与「共 25 条」「第 1/2 页」 |
| 标记已读 | ✅ 用例 4 断言精确 POST `/api/messages/7/read`（兼验 ID 归一化）；用例 5 负断言已读行不重复 POST |
| 审批类消息跳对应单据 | ✅ 用例 4 真实断言 `lastLoc === "/production?mo=SC20260916001"`；用例 5 断言 `/material-issues?doc=LL20260916002` |
| 与铃铛弹层数据同源 | ✅ 同一 messagesApi；未读数共享 queryKey `messages-unread-count`；标记已读 invalidate `messages-page`/`messages-unread-count`/`messages-panel` 三 key |
| 未注册路由 MENU_PATHS 裁决 | ✅ `/replenishments`、`/bom-setup` 确未注册（menu.ts `M()` 无 path)，用例 6/7 断言 toast 记参数 + `lastLoc` 留在 `/messages`，不静默断链 |

约束核查：无 em/en dash ✅；无 emoji ✅；图标全部 @phosphor-icons ✅；无新弹窗（弹窗四律 N/A);Skeleton/DocToast 只消费未改 ✅;`web/` 与后端零改动 ✅；视觉 hex 全部命中 future.css `[data-theme="future"]` 令牌（唯一例外 `#9aa5b1`，见 ⚠️)。

## 发现

**Critical：无。**

**Important 1 — 经理反审核批准动作在 web2 无处可用，报告理由不实。** web2 全工程 grep 无 `approveUnapproveRequest`/`rejectUnapproveRequest`,`productionApi` 只有 `requestUnapprove`(endpoints.ts:112);ProductionPage 仅有「申请反审核」和「反审核申请中…待经理批准」徽标（ProductionPage.tsx:1274)，没有任何同意/拒绝入口。报告声称「审批动作走 /production 单据页本身」不成立——跳单据页后经理无法完成审批。按 brief 清单该抽屉不在交付范围内，**defer 本身可接受**，但必须登记为后续批次的明确缺口（否则审批流在 web2 断链）。连带：`ProductionPage.tsx:1476` 注释「提交后经理在消息中心批准/拒绝」对 web2 已过时，应改。

**Minor 1 — 分页 select 的 Tailwind 类是死类。** `.f-input` 无层叠且带 `height:44px`(future.css:153-155，注释自述优先级高于 Tailwind 工具类），因此 `MessagesPage.tsx:875` 的 `h-9 px-2 text-sm` 均不生效，select 实际 44px 高于旁边 h-9(36px）按钮。width 修复本身正确（w-24 定宽容器包裹，不动 future.css，登录页等既有输入框零影响 ✅)，仅高度不齐，视觉小瑕疵。

**Minor 2 — 老工程对照面。** `web/src/__tests__/messages.test.ts` 仅 1 个 API 形状用例；新 8 个用例在覆盖面（分页/筛选/分流/裁决/铃铛）上远超老工程，属增强而非对齐缺口。

## ⚠️ 项

- **MaterialIssuePage `?doc=` 深链越 brief 清单**:13 行，渲染期派生 + effect 清参，与 ProductionPage `?mo=`(ProductionPage.tsx:830-840）逐行同构；brief 接口要求「跳对应单据页」，无此深链则领料审批消息无处可跳，判定**合理必需**，报告已自报，接受。
- **`#9aa5b1` 不在 future.css 令牌中**，但系全工程 24 文件（含 LoginPage/MainLayout）的既有惯例灰，非本任务引入，不算 Task 11 违规，建议批次收尾时统一收进令牌。
- **测试抽查（5/8)**：用例 1(query 参数+同源未读数）、3（翻页 page=2 + 切 50 回 page=1)、4（精确 POST URL + 深链 lastLoc + 未读数重拉 >1 次）、5（负断言不发 POST + 深链）、8（铃铛跳 /messages）均为真实断言，无摆设测试。
- MainLayout diff 上下文可见 `/purchase-returns` loader 复用 PurchaseReceiptPage — 非本任务引入，仅提示后续核对。

## 质量结论

**通过，可合并。** 行为照抄老系统忠实（含 `slice(0,19).replace("T"," ")`、ID 归一化、类型分流口径），裁决模式与 Task 9/10 惯例一致，测试真实且实测 8/8 绿。唯一实质缺口是经理反审核批准动作整体未落地（Important 1)，属范围外 defer，但必须挂账并修正 ProductionPage.tsx:1476 的过时注释，否则 web2 审批流闭环断裂这一点会被遗忘。
